<!-- Plan of record for the board engine: every netlist part is a node in one pipeline. Line references are against the tree at the time of writing (branch feat/p2-qemu-node, 2026-09-23). -->

# Plan: every component a node

## 1. The model

Every fitted part contributes to the one quasi-static nodal solve that already exists (`board/src/cluster.rs` `QuasiStaticMna`: Norton-stamped Thevenin sources, dense elimination; R1, R16). Resistors, closed switch poles and on-state channels are conductances; rails are declared terminals — a source referenced to the regulator's own ground pin, entering every dependent solve as a constant; pads are Thevenin sources at the device's real strength; diodes, LEDs, BJTs, FETs and the CCR are piecewise-linear elements whose region is chosen by a bounded, ordered flip loop inside one solve, cold-started every time; capacitors are single-pole closed forms. A solve runs only when an input to a cluster changes and yields the operating point at that instant. Time enters in exactly one way: a node with capacitance publishes `Settling { v0, v_inf, tau, t0_ns, level }`, analog senses evaluate v(now) at read time, and the instants at which a digital sense would flip and at which the exponential is within one quantum of v∞ are computed in closed form and armed on the wheel as integer nanoseconds. Nothing is integrated per tick (R8, R11). Deliberately not SPICE: no timestep, no Newton on exponential laws, no inductor dynamics (L stays a DC short), no multi-pole transients (one pole per resistive block, stated), no ground the harness did not declare (R18), no parasitics the netlist or scenario does not name. The `ClusterSolver` seam remains the escape hatch (R21); the decision record's revisit trigger is not pulled.

One more rule, from the user, that shapes everything below: **there is one pipeline** — netlist part → registry class → node. No stub tier, no facade-only part, no allow-list. A part is a node whose class has behaviour, or the board refuses to build naming the part and its value. §3 says what that deletes.

## 2. Taxonomy

| Class | In the model | State | Set from | Runtime cost |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Resistor | conductance edge (today, `system.rs:824-846`). `Pulled(level, ohms)` carries the **winning source's series path** (rule 2), not `edge_total_ohms` (`engine.rs:1236`, the 82 200 Ω artefact). No R_th solve on the fast path: the true R_th comes from `thevenin_at(node)` only inside an already-escalated solve (a reviewer is right that no non-test consumer reads the payload and that an R_th keyed on every slot impedance would re-deliver senses the ROM boot keeps stable today) | ohms, mutable via `SetEdge` (phase 7 only) | value; tolerant first-token parser (62/66 EC32 caps and all four inductors parse to `None` today, `registry.rs:435-485`) | 0 on the fast path |
| Capacitor | annotation on its node, **never a union-find edge** (that grows the EC32 GND/VDD cluster 8→26 nets). Cap to a terminal = per-node pole, τ = R_th·C. Both ends on terminals (decoupling, 55 EC32 caps) = static, skipped. Parallel caps lump. A **symmetric differential pair** (two nodes of equal R_th sharing a cross-cap, the ADC filter shape) decomposes exactly into two single poles, τ_cm = R·C_cm and τ_diff = R·(C_cm + 2·C_d), and is modelled as such. Any other second cap in one block, or a cap on a node that hosts a PWL element or a non-terminal PWL edge, raises `Finding::MultiPoleBlock` and stays DC-open (declaration order) — the PWL-on-a-settling-node case is the back door to transient device simulation and is closed by rule, not by code | per node `{v0, v_inf, tau, t0_ns, level}` + solve generation, engine-side | value; `Harness::capacitor(net, pf)`; `PinDecl::capacitance_pf` opt-in | one `ln_q` per capacitive node per solve; ≤ 2 wheel entries per capacitive node per solve |
| Inductor | DC short: a build-time **identity union** of its two nets, a closed pole's semantics (phase 4 — as a 0 Ω conduction edge it made a regulator's rail a member of every load's cluster; as a union the rail *is* the regulator's terminal node) | — | — | 0 |
| Diode / LED | PWL: off = `GMIN` leakage (1 GΩ, so every element terminal stays source-reachable and every region test is evaluable — INFINITY would leave the far side Floating and the test with no operand); on = Vf source + r_d edge. LED `lit` = branch current ≥ I_on, via `BuiltSystem::branch_current("D3")` | region bit | part → `pwl_library` with provenance keyed on the netlist's **Manufacturer_Part_Number** (`LTST-C190KGKT` for the 21 Edge LEDs whose value is the bare `LED`; `White` for D601/D602 from the vendor BOM; `SS36`), else the value; no resolvable Vf = build error naming the part, like any other unmodelled part | ≤ 2N solves in its cluster, only when a dirty pass flips a region |
| FET / BJT (Q1, U3, U401) | PWL 3-terminal **with the body diode** (P-FET: D→S) as a PWL branch: BJT = b-e diode + c-e switch when I_b ≥ I_load/hFE(min) from the spec (an under-driven base reads as a sagging collector, not a clean switch); FET d-s = R_ds(on) when V_gs beyond V_th, else GMIN. A region test on a genuinely Floating control terminal evaluates as off. Control pin joins by a GMIN membership edge (gate in-cluster, no cross-cluster dirty) | region bits | `register_pwl(key, PwlSpec)` | as diode |
| Switch / jumper | `PartClass::Switch { poles: Vec<{a, b, state}> }` by **pin id**; a closed pole is a **build-time identity union** (`pin_short` semantics, honouring `detached`), an open pole nothing — no resolver change, no ∞ Ω edge, no `SetEdge`. Jumpers are one-pole switches (`Scenario::jumper` stays as an alias of `Scenario::switch`); 3-pin jumpers = two poles, fixing JP1's latent throw-to-throw short (`system.rs:1071-1078`). Auto tier for 2-pin `SW_*` (SW1, `SW_Push`). S301's `<n>_ON/<n>_OFF` pairing is a registry declaration — the netlist carries none. J101's solder link is a one-pole switch, open. Live toggling is cut: nothing on any board toggles a switch after start (`grep SW1 board/tests` hits only pin tables), the QEMU node cannot act on RESN anyway (`ffi.rs` has no reset entry), and identity union is what the ROM boot relies on today | pole states at build | `register_switch`, `Scenario::switch(ref, pole, state)` | 0 |
| Connector | `Boundary` node (unchanged, `system.rs:888`): pins are pass-through terminals the harness attaches to; an unattached pin is open. EC32 J203, J301 (+ card model), Edge J1–J24, DS2 J1–J5 | — | auto (`Conn`/`Screw_Terminal`/`J`/`P` prefix) or `register_boundary` | 0 |
| Test point | `Probe` node: one pin, senses, never drives; `BuiltSystem::probe("TP3")` reads it. No board carries one today; the class exists so `TP` never means "absent" | — | auto (`TestPoint`/`TP`) | 0 |
| Mechanical | `Mechanical` node: pads recorded, nothing electrical; a pad sharing a net with a driver raises `Finding::MechanicalOnDrivenNet` (phase 4, with the other build lints). Edge H5–H8 (`MountingHole_Pad`), EC32 J701/J702 (`Mounting Hole Vss`), `PCB` (no nodes), `NC_Net` (one layout node) — the last three registered by value, since the transcribed netlist has no libsource and their prefixes match no table entry | — | auto (`MountingHole`/`Logo`/`Fiducial`/`H`/`MK`) or `register_mechanical(value)` | 0 |
| Crystal / oscillator | `Oscillator` node: VCC/GND PowerIn; OUT drives a `Drive::Periodic` at the frequency parsed from the value (`TG2520SMN 20.0000M-…` → 20 MHz) **as a rate** when VCC is up — the encoding a step clock uses, one drive, zero per-edge cost (R7). The rate crosses a capacitor by the AC rule (X100.OUT → C132 → U101.2A on the EC32); DC resolution keeps it open | rate | value | one publish |
| Logic gate (74LVC2G04 ×2, SN74LVC1G14 ×21) | `LogicGate` node: A sensed, Y driven at datasheet R_out with t_pd as a scheduled instant (cited); Schmitt inputs use the datasheet hysteresis. A channel whose input carries a **rate** is in rate mode: it re-publishes the rate on Y (PulseSink → PulseSource) and drives Y at the rate's average (VCC·duty) through R_out, level sense ignored — so U101's self-biased stage (R101 100 kΩ from 2Y to 2A) solves to its mid-rail fixed point in one pass with no sense→drive iteration (§12 item 5 changed this: the channel relays the input's segment between its datasheet ports, no average drive, and only where the segment's phases cross the input's thresholds or on a self-biased input — an inverting stage with a plain input and a resistor from its own output, as on U101's second stage; the periodic drive task, the cleanup and the final pass) | last level / rate | datasheet | one drive per input transition |
| Regulator / rail | `Rail` node: IN a `PowerIn` sense, EN a `DigitalIn` sense; OUT is a **declared terminal** (rule 1) driving `{V(ref) + v_set, z_out}` from t_up = t(IN ≥ UVLO ∧ EN) + t_SS (datasheet soft-start, a scheduled wake — the ramp shape is not modelled, the step is a real instant); down = **released** (a real buck/LDO output is high-Z, so a bench strap onto a down rail sources it instead of fighting it) plus `Finding::RailDown { part, reason }` at build; a 0 V source only for parts with an active output discharge, cited. v_set: from the value suffix for fixed parts (`XL1509-5V`, `XL1509-3.3V`, `LDO 300mA, 3.3V`), or from the FB divider the netlist carries, read once at attach through a build-time topology query `ComponentNetIo::resistors_at(pin)` — `V_FB·(1 + R_top/R_bot)`, 0.8 V·(1 + 13.3/10.5) = 1.813 V for U402 (Common_VDD), 0.8 V·(1 + 37.4/10.5) = 3.649 V for U403 (Common_LDOin) — so two `DCDC 3A SOT563` with one registry key come out different, as they are. `ref` = the part's own GND pin, read through the sense it has and added to the published voltage (the engine's terminal holds absolute volts); a ref no source reaches → the output stays released, `Finding::RailDown { reason: ReferenceUnheld }`. Isolated DC/DC (UCC12040): VISO **and** GNDS are terminals; GNDS is never driven by the part — the isolated domain's reference is whatever the board or the harness ties it to (rule 6, no implicit ground), so a domain nothing ties has no reference and its rail stays down, named (phase 4 decided this in place of "pinned at 0 V of its own frame"). IN/EN are `PowerIn`/`DigitalIn` senses, not `Analog` ones: the same published state, and no solve forced on the input rail's cluster every pass (the ROM boot's 0-escalation budget). Current limit is cut: a sagging rail is not a terminal, and a non-terminal rail unions every load into exactly the 26/47-net cluster rule 1 exists to prevent. z_out is recorded for the I-V port, not solved. NaN (`system.rs:1130`) has no survivor: every PowerOut is a modelled rail | up/down | datasheet v_set, z_out, UVLO, t_SS | one drive + one wake per state change |
| Ground | not implicit (R18): the bench return is a harness `power(0V)` terminal; `net_stuck GND 0` stays valid scenario | — | harness | 0 |
| Supervisor (STM1061) | VCC `PowerIn` sense on **Common_VDD** (U404 VCC is the core rail in `p2_ec32mb.net`, not VIO); ~OUT `DigitalOut` idle Released; a **comparator with hysteresis** — sinks 0 V @ R_ol while V(VCC) < V_TH (1.6 V), releases at V_TH + V_HYST, both cited from the STM1061 electrical characteristics. No t_rec: the STM1061 is a voltage detector, not a reset supervisor (a reviewer is right; the draft's `t_rec` had no provenance and the part has none); the two propagation delays it does name, `t_PD` 25 µs and `t_PR` 30 µs, are scheduled instants, and below the 0.7 V the output is guaranteed from it is released, not asserted (phase 4) | 1 bit | datasheet | one drive per crossing |
| Digital IC pin | `DigitalOut`/`DigitalBidir` at datasheet impedance (already per drive, `engine.rs:665-690`) | last drive | PinDecl | one drive per input transition |
| Open-drain pin | not a new PinKind: released `DigitalOut` + resistor to a terminal (R19, `vo2631.rs:41-50`). `PinDecl::idle: Option<TheveninDrive>` (`None` = Released, `Some` = any static level and impedance) removes the release-in-attach ritual for **static** idle drives; the second build pass **stays** for the rest (§5). Native firmware: `GpioChannelConfig.open_drain` (sink or release, never push-pull) | — | PinDecl / HAL table | 0 |
| Memory (PSRAM ×4) | `Psram` model (APS6404L QSPI, `models/src/psram.rs`): CE# sensed, SIO released while deselected, the command set answered from an array — the SPI NOR model's shift engine parameterised. The ROM and firmware never touch it; the user's rule says that is not a reason for it to be a facade | array + phase | datasheet | one drive per SIO transition |
| Opto (6N137, VO2631 ×4) | one `Opto` model: LED = PWL branch owned by the part (the transducer primitive, BOARD_ENGINE.md:225), output = open-collector sink while I_LED ≥ I_TH and VCC up (the VO2631 model's rule) | region + 1 bit | datasheet | as diode |
| Analog front end | Analog senses unchanged; populated filter caps → Settling (differential-pair case); REFP/REFN read for ratiometric codes; bridge legs = four edges set by the plant via `SetEdge` (phase 7) | edge values | Config / plant | one value-only edge update per sample |
| MCU node (P2) | `P2Package<Core>`: the package (64 pads, VDD/VIO PowerIn, RESN sensed, XI a sense whose square wave's rate is the crystal the PLL multiplies — replacing `with_crystal_hz`, XO a released output; §12 item 5's final pass: through XI's thresholds in the clock mode the chip starts in, and any running segment once the core's clock word turns XI's 1 MΩ feedback on) around a core: QEMU (`p2-qemu`), the ISS (`SIL/p2iss`), or the native MCU (`McuComponent`, today's `P2EdgeModule` minus its facade). Pad = Thevenin from WRPIN bits 13:11 (OUT=1) / 10:8 (OUT=0): FAST → `P2_FAST_OHMS`, 17.99 Ω, the least-squares fit of the datasheet's `Voh`/`Vol` rows (DC Characteristics, p. 48); 1K5/15K/150K → R; FLOAT → None. The current-source modes (1MA/100UA/10UA) are not mapped — no caller, and the datasheet names their currents (p. 2, p. 24) but no compliance, which `Drive::Current` would need (§12 item 5, the P2 task). **IN reflects the net for every pad whose published drive is None or ≥ `WEAK_DRIVE_OHMS`**; a FAST pad keeps reading its own OUT (what p2core does, `board.rs:482-484`, and what keeps the 60 000-state differential identical). A **START gate** replaces "pads release when the rail is down" (phase 4): the package holds `P2Core::start` and every wake the core routes through `P2Pads` until `RESN` reads released and `VDD` reads a voltage inside the datasheet's 1.7–1.9 V window (P2X8C4M64P Datasheet, DC Characteristics, p. 47), starts the core at that instant and lands the held wakes there; clock segment 0 begins at it (the QEMU core anchors its RCFAST segment there in `start`). The handle reads `StartState::{Held { reset }, Started { at_ns }}`. The datasheet's 3 ms restart after a `RESn` rise (Pin Descriptions, p. 6) is counted on a wake of the package's own: the reset releases when `RESN` reads released with `VDD` inside the window, START is 3 ms later if it stays released, and every core's wakes — through `P2Pads` or the net I/O the package hands it — go through the package's `WakeGate` (§12 item 5, the P2 task). **A pad drives high at its bank's supply**: the package senses the sixteen `VIO_a_b` pins into a `BankSupplies` table a core reads when it publishes a pad (`BankSupplies::pad_drive`); a bank whose pin names no voltage drives nothing, high or low, and is reported once by its supply pin. A `VDD` that leaves its window mid-run with `RESN` not asserted is reported as `StartState::BrownoutWithoutReset` and holds the core (`P2Core::reset`: the QEMU core runs no further slice, the native core says it cannot stop), never a pad change; an engine `Finding` for it waits for a model-side report path this plan does not have (§12 item 5, the P2 task) | `published: [Option<Option<TheveninDrive>>; 64]` | WRPIN | one drive per pad change |

Every pin is also an I-V port: `PinHandle::sense_current()` / `ComponentNetIo::on_branch(pin, cb)` report the current into the pin from the same solve. It is an Analog-sense-equivalent bench instrument: subscribing one escalates its cluster (only an escalated cluster has node voltages), and it is refused on terminal pins, whose current spans clusters.

### Three rules the taxonomy rests on

**1. Terminals are declared, and they are cluster boundaries.** A terminal is a root held by a *declared* source — a `PowerOut` pin, a harness `power(V)` endpoint, a `net_stuck` — never a root inferred from a drive's impedance (`PinHandle::set_drive` accepts any impedance per call, `component.rs:386-400`, so an impedance-keyed boundary would flip mid-run while membership is fixed at build). Each terminal is its own one-node cluster whose state is assigned once (two disagreeing terminal sources on it → Contention, once, not per dependent), and `build_topology` records a `terminal → dependent clusters` fan-out that `set_drive`/`dirty_scope` consult when a rail changes — so `resolve_dirty == full pass` survives, and the incremental-vs-full property test (`engine.rs:4877-4926`) gains random terminal drive changes as phase 4's first commit. Edges and caps touch a terminal, nothing unions through it, its voltage enters the solve as a constant. This is Dirichlet elimination for terminals, which `cluster.rs:52-64` rejected for *all* ideal sources; the revision is stated there: it is safe because a terminal's own contention is decided before any dependent solve runs, and it is gated on `nominal_analog_cluster` and `net_stuck_shared_node` passing at 1 µV without re-blessing. What the rule prevents: with D/LED as edges and no boundary, `mad_edge.net` collapses +3.3V, +5V, GND and all 21 LED chains into one **47-net** cluster — the chains are inverter Y → 220 Ω → anode → cathode on GND, so it is GND that grows — and any Analog sense in it force-escalates every P-pin edge (`engine.rs:1191-1197`).

**2. Source-strength projection, in one form.** Today two driving slots on one root with different levels are Contention regardless of impedance (`levels_of`, `engine.rs:1017-1029`, `:1219-1224`), so a P2 pad publishing `{3.3 V, 15 kΩ}` against any sink is Contention. The replacement ranks every source reaching a root by **total ohms** = slot impedance + series path from the cached `dist` matrix (a terminal: path only). A source with total ≥ `WEAK_DRIVE_OHMS = STREAM_COLLAPSE_THRESHOLD` (1 kΩ) is a *pull* and never contends. Among the rest: a source ≥ `ESCALATION_IMPEDANCE_RATIO` (10×) the strongest loses — the state is the strongest's, and because it lost through less than 1 kΩ it is a fight, so `Finding::Contention` names both (the flash-vs-card case on P58: the card reaches through 25 + 240 = 265 Ω ≥ 250, node ≈ 3.0 V, reads the flash's level, finding raised — the draft's "Contention" was wrong and the reviewer right). Disagreeing sources within 10× **solve** (m ≤ 2 in every case on these boards), publish the divided voltage, and project it through the dead band `V_IL/V_IH = 0.8/2.0 V` (JESD8C.01, the 3.3 V LVCMOS standard — JESD8-7A is the 1.8 V one; a net-level constant until a PinDecl carries its own): inside the band → Contention + `AmbiguousLevel`; outside → Analog(v) + `Finding::Contention`. Two 25 Ω push-pulls still sit at 1.65 V, inside the band, so every existing Contention holds; a driver against a terminal on its own root stays Analog(rail) as today (the firmware must *see* a short-to-rail, not hold its last level) and gains the finding it lacks. Otherwise the winner sets the state: a strong slot on the root → `Driven(level)`; a terminal on the root → `Analog(v)` (`an_injected_short_outvotes_a_driver_reached_through_resistance` keeps its precedence); anything else → `Pulled(level, ohms)` where ohms is the winner's series path, and a *weak* slot's own impedance is part of that path — a 15 kΩ pad is a resistor to its rail, a 25 Ω pad is a driver. So the 47 Ω single edge stays `Pulled(_, 47)` bit-identical (`a_driven_level_crosses_a_series_resistor`, `engine.rs:3672-3706`), the lone I2C pad reads `Pulled(High, 15000)`, and the unstuck-GND chain reports the path that wins, not 82 200. Escalation trigger 2 (`engine.rs:1186-1197`, both levels present anywhere in the cluster) retires in favour of this ranking.

**3. Membership is fixed at build; the only mutable conductance is the plant's.** Switch poles and jumpers are build-time identity unions (rule above). The one runtime-mutable edge is the bridge leg the plant sets through `Command::SetEdge { edge, ohms }` — value-only, never a `topology_version` bump, refreshing that cluster's `dist` locally — and it lands in phase 7 with its first real consumer. The invariant that wins is "clusters are electrically independent, dirty scope is exact" (`engine.rs:949-958`); there is no epoch machinery and `subscribe_topology` (`engine.rs:2915-2920`) stays the deferred seam it is.

## 3. One pipeline

A netlist part is **either** a node whose class has behaviour **or** the board refuses to build with `RegistryError::UnknownPart { reference, part, value }` (the error gains `value` — on the transcribed EC32 netlist `part` is empty and the message would name nothing). The pipeline is `classify()` → `PartClass` → node; the classes are exactly the taxonomy rows: `Passive`, `Switch`, `Pwl`, `Boundary`, `Probe`, `Mechanical`, `Registered` (a model constructor: `Rail`, `Oscillator`, `LogicGate`, `Supervisor`, `Opto`, `Psram`, `P2Package`, and the models that exist). A connector is a pass-through node, a test point a probe node, a mounting hole or board outline an explicit `Mechanical` node, an IC nobody has modelled a build error. There is no allow-list and no "declare its outputs as inputs so it cannot contend" rule.

| U1, U2 `XL1509` | stub | `Rail` (fixed, from `-5V`/`-3.3V`) |
| U3 `APM4953` | stub + `pin_short` | `Pwl` P-FET with body diode |
| Q1 `2N3904` | stub / `npn_switch` model | `Pwl` BJT |
| SW1 `SW_Push` | stub, 2 passive pins | `Switch` (auto, `SW_` prefix), open |
| JP1 `Jumper_3_Open`; JP2–JP5 | jumpers | `Switch` (two poles; one pole) |
| J1–J24 | boundary | `Boundary` |
| H5–H8 `MountingHole_Pad` | ignored (absent) | `Mechanical` |
| D1, D2 `SS36`; D3–D25 `LED` | DC-open | `Pwl` diode; `Pwl` LED keyed on `LTST-C190KGKT` |

The DS2 addon has no stub today (U1 is the ADS122U04 model; the rest are auto) and changes only in naming. The census tests change with the pipeline: `ec32mb_module` asserts 20 model nodes + 2 primitive nodes (S301, U401), `edgeboard` 38 + 11 (SW1, Q1, U3, IC6–IC13) — not the 47/20 a reviewer estimated, because the eight CCRs become elements too; `component_refs()` becomes `nodes()` returning `(ref, class)` so both counts are asserted. A `stub_count` census is a CI gate from phase 1: it must never rise, and it is 0 on all three boards when `stub.rs` is deleted in phase 4.

## 4. Interfaces

**I2C.** No I2C device exists on any MaD board (NavKey header J19 only, no pull-ups in `mad_edge.net`; `jm_i2c` uncalled; `peripherals/src/i2c.rs` a no-op). A reviewer is right that this gives phase 6 no board consumer; it is kept because it is the question this plan answers, as a **bench** fixture. Two things had to be true and were not. First, rule 2: `jm_i2c` PU_15K publishes `{3.3 V, 15 kΩ}` on a pad the driver **never releases** — it drives high through the pull mode (`wrpin pullup` then `drvh`, `jm_i2c.spin2:57-65`) and samples with `testp` — so alone the pad projects `Pulled(High, 15000)`, a slave sinking 0 V @ 30 Ω wins (15 k ≥ 10 × 30) → `Driven(Low)`, no matrix. Second, the node's read: with `sensed()` = `(dir & out) | (!dir & in_ext)` (`p2-qemu/src/lib.rs:398-401`) every ACK would read NAK and a stretching slave would be invisible; the fix in §2 (IN = net for weak or released pads) is what makes the clock-stretch wait `drvh scl … testp scl / if_nc jmp $-1` (`:113-116`) and the ACK sample (`:146-151`) work. Two masters resolve by whichever sinks, and the one reading Low while driving high through 15 kΩ backs off. Rise time only with `Harness::capacitor("SDA", 100)`: 15 kΩ·100 pF crosses 1.5 V at τ·ln(3.3/1.8) = 0.606τ = 910 ns. `bridge_i2c` on native firmware is cut: `jm_i2c` has no caller. New `board/tests/i2c_bus.rs` with a bench `i2c_slave.rs`; no existing test changes.

**SPI (EC32MB).** S301 becomes a switch: `rom_boot_ec32mb.rs:166-172` drops both `pin_short`s for `.switch("EC32.S301", 2/4, Closed)`; SPI_CS is identity-merged with P2_IO61 through the closed pole and reads `Driven(Low)` from P61 with no projection change; `unmodeled_oscillator_and_open_dip_switch_float_their_nets` (`ec32mb_module.rs:556-583`) flips to `Pulled(High/Low, 10500)` by pole, and its XI half to "XI carries a rate". P58 is a three-source node; a flash-vs-card fight is `Driven(flash level)` with `Finding::Contention` (rule 2). Firmware observes nothing new on a healthy board; the ROM boot compares no golden trace.

**UART.** Unchanged by default: no netlist carries signal-line capacitance and pad capacitance is opt-in, so `a_firmware_byte_crosses_the_net_as_edges` (`serial_levels.rs:597-609`) and the `serial_levels` golden hold. With 100 pF declared on TX (τ = 2.5 ns at 25 Ω) the rising edge crosses 1.5 V at 2.5·ln(3.3/1.8) = 1.515 ns and the falling at 2.5·ln(3.3/1.5) = 1.97 ns — **+2/+2 ns** on the integer grid, not the draft's +1.5/+2.0 (a reviewer is right; the phase-5 test asserts instants from the engine's own rounding function); a new test asserts the byte still decodes. The "missing pull-up reads a permanent start bit" bench symptom is reproduced only by `Harness::leak(net, volts, ohms)` — an explicit weak source in scenario, never invented (R2).

**Step/dir + isolator.** STEP stays a rate (R7; 820 k edges/s is measured infeasible). DIR/ENA unchanged. Q1 → PWL NPN: `Net-(Q1-B)` reads Analog(≈0.7) not `Pulled(Low, 43000)` — `the_enable_path_crosses_the_isolator_and_the_transistor` (`isolation_bridge.rs:763-810`) updates, and asserts SC_ENA's *voltage* with the drive's opto input in the harness (series R + LED to its 5 V, bench): I_b = (V_OUTC − 0.7)/43 kΩ ≈ 60–100 µA against a ~10 mA opto load leaves a 2N3904 β-limited, and the model says so rather than reading a clean switch. JP1 gets a real two-pole position. RELAY_EVENT_CEILING=32 unaffected.

**Encoder.** U25 inputs are already Analog; no change. Velocity pacing and propagation delay stay out.

**Analog (ADS122U04).** DNP by default → nothing changes. With R6/R7 + C7..C9 populated (values declared by the test, the netlist says `X`): C7/C9 are the common-mode caps and C8 the differential one — the standard ADC filter, where the differential cap is normally ~10× the others and sets the pole the converter actually sees. The symmetric-pair case gives τ_cm = R·C_cm and τ_diff = R·(C_cm + 2·C_d): with C_cm = 100 nF and C_d = 1 µF, τ_diff = 4.7 kΩ·2.1 µF = 9.87 ms, ~20× the draft's 470 µs (a reviewer is right; the draft dropped the dominant pole). The same applies to C2 across REFP/REFN. RDATA at a conversion instant reads v(now); `rdata_returns_the_conversion_for_the_driven_bridge_differential` gains a 5τ_diff settle and a mid-exponential case; the 1e-6 V asserts get a stated tolerance.

**Power, enable, brownout, reset, clock.** Regulators drive real volts, so bench straps become ideal-vs-rail Contention (as `ds2_regressions.rs:250-276` already shows). Tests that encode stubs, and what each becomes: `bench_straps_clear_the_domains_they_feed` (its `dark` half inverts: U3's body diode lifts V_IN without the FET scenario), `a_bare_board_reports_only_the_domains_that_arrive_over_a_cable`, `the_assembled_machine_leaves_only_the_unplugged_ports_unsourced`, `the_whole_machine_builds_without_contention` — the on-board straps go, J2 `power(12V)/power(0V)` and the servo-side J21 endpoints (a domain the drive supplies) remain; `the_bridged_tx_pin_is_the_only_driver` → `every_driver_is_a_registered_model` (TX, X100, U101, U601, U404); `the_reset_node_is_pulled_up_and_floats_without_the_pull_up` → a POR sequence: `Driven(Low)` while Common_VDD < 1.6 V, `Pulled(High, 10500)` from t_SS(U402) (RESN carries no capacitor, so no RC delay); the two census tests (§3). IC14's orphan GND2 surfaces as `UnreferencedDomain` once ISO67xx reads VCC2−GND2 instead of V(VCC2) (`iso67xx.rs:111-115`). The P2 starts at the START-gate instant; the icount node's clock comes from XI.

- `board/src/registry.rs`: tolerant `parse_passive_value`; `Classification::{Switch, Mechanical, Probe}` checked in `classify()` before the value lookup (`:262-264`, S301 has no libsource); `register_switch`, `register_pwl`, `register_rail`, `register_mechanical`; auto `SW_*`; `UnknownPart` gains `value`; delete `Stubbed`, `Ignored`, `ExpansionHook`.

- `board/src/system.rs`: `Scenario::switch`; `Harness::capacitor`, `Harness::leak`; closed poles → identity unions honouring `detached`; `add_capacitor` (annotation), `add_pwl`; `PowerOut` → terminal + `ref_root`; `ComponentNetIo::resistors_at`. The second build pass (`:483-506`) **stays** and becomes a bounded fixed point: replay recorded attach drives, re-resolve, repeat while they change, bound = declared power-tree depth, `Finding::BuildNotSettled` past it. A reviewer is right that deleting it was wrong: the GPIO bridge drives its power-on level at attach (`mcu.rs:1189-1193`), the ISO67xx drives at its *sensed* rail (`iso67xx.rs:729-741`), and the EC32 tree is J203 → U401 → U402.IN → Common_LDOin → U501..U508 EN/IN, three senses deep. Rails with t_SS > 0 are down in the build snapshot — honestly, since no wake has run — and `powered_module()` becomes build + start + step to t_SS. New invariant, asserted on Edge and EC32MB: the build snapshot equals the live system's state before its first wake.
- `board/src/component.rs`: `PinDecl::idle: Option<TheveninDrive>`, `PinDecl::capacitance_pf`, constructors; `sense_current`, `on_branch`, `edge(a, b) -> EdgeHandle`. `PinKind` untouched.
- `board/src/cluster.rs`: `Cluster { …, pwl, capacitors, edge_ohms }`, `ClusterInputs { sources, terminals, edge_ohms }` — **no regions**: every solve cold-starts with all elements off; `ClusterSolution { node_states, regions, branch_currents }`; terminal stamps; one-at-a-time flip loop; `thevenin_at(node)`; `GMIN`. Trait stays `&self`. The Dirichlet revision stated in the module doc.
- `board/src/engine.rs`: terminals as one-node clusters and the fan-out table in `build_topology` (`:769-871`); rule 2 replacing `levels_of`/contention/trigger 2/state assignment (`:1017-1029`, `:1075-1116`, `:1205-1241`); per-cluster `dynamics`; `TimerTarget::{Crossing, SettleEnd} { cluster, generation }` through `arm`/`fire_due_timers`/`next_virtual_deadline` (`:1962-2432`, `:2768`); `resolve_reference` extended; `Command::SetEdge` in phase 7.
- `board/src/net.rs`: `Settling`, `level_of` over it, `volts_at(state, now)`, `WEAK_DRIVE_OHMS`, `V_IL`/`V_IH`, `ln_q`. `board/src/event_log.rs`: `Crossing`, `SettleEnd`, `RegionFlip`, `EdgeSet`; `Finding::{NonConvergent, MultiPoleBlock, UnreferencedDomain, UndecoupledPowerPin, RailDown, BuildNotSettled}` — `BrownoutWithoutReset` declined: the package reports a brownout on its handle and holds the core (§12 item 5, the P2 task's decision (8)).
- `board/src/mcu.rs`: `GpioChannelConfig.open_drain`.
- `models/src/`: `regulator.rs`, `supervisor.rs`, `logic_gate.rs`, `oscillator.rs`, `psram.rs`, `opto.rs`, `pwl_library.rs` (provenance per entry), bench `i2c_slave.rs`; the NSI50010/NPN chain trick (`isolation/mod.rs:46-52`) retires into elements and their 6.92 Ω / 100 Ω tunings to the 10× rule are re-derived from datasheets.
- `boards/src/`: `p2.rs` (`P2Package`), `ec32mb.rs` rewritten to classes, `stub.rs` deleted.
- `p2-qemu/src/lib.rs`: `pad_drive(pin)`; `sensed()` per §2; XI `PulseSink`; START gate; `wrpin` routes through `recompute_and_mark`. WRPIN edges are stamped at the executing cog's clock once the vtable's `wrpin` carries a cog (the target is beside this crate in MaD `SIL/qemu-p2/`, and knows the cog at the call); until then they are stamped at `machine_now_ns()` and the caveat is documented. `SIL/p2core/src/board.rs:482-484` takes the same `sensed()` rule; `SIL/p2iss` takes the XI sink and loses its fiat.
- `board/tests/machine_parts/mod.rs`, `boards/tests/ec32mb.rs`: stubs and placeholders replaced by classes.

## 6. Cost model

Solves per event are unchanged: one `resolve_dirty` of the touched cluster per applied drive (`engine.rs:2291-2361`), on the fast path unless two sources within 10× disagree (an m ≤ 2 solve) or an Analog sense is present. Additions are bounded per cluster: rule 2 is an O(sources) ranking per dirty root from cached `dist`; PWL adds ≤ 2N solves only in a dirty cluster containing elements (cold start makes that the bound every time, and at m ≤ 8 it is ~170 multiply-adds per solve); a settle adds one `ln_q` per capacitive node and at most two wheel entries; there is no R_th solve on the fast path. Rails add no solves of their own — terminals are constants — and a rail change costs one fan-out walk; a rail whose feedback divider hangs between its output and its ground solves that divider's two-root cluster once when the rail rises (rule 2: two comparable sources, the rail through R_top and ground through R_bot), which is the ROM boot's two at t_SS (phase 4).

Cluster size is the decision. Rule 1 keeps every pull-up a 2-node cluster and every LED chain {Y, anode} plus terminals; identity unions keep S301 at zero cost; today's largest clusters (EC32 8 nets across GND/Common_VDD/LDOin, Edge 11 across +3.3V/LED anodes — offline sizing) split at their rails. Decoupling caps contribute nothing. Expected max m ≤ 8 on all three boards; phase 0 measures, phase 4 bounds (phase 3 measured 29 / 10 bare and 20 on the Edge `+3.3V` cluster under the rails — the resistor edges' unions through terminals, phase 4's to cut; see the phase-3 review record). On the ROM boot every SPI edge is a 25 Ω pad against 10.5 kΩ to a VIO terminal (10 500 ≥ 1 kΩ: a pull), so no matrix is built today and none after; the projection gains one compare per source pair. Expect the 16.9 k edges within noise of 0.2 s (≈12 µs/edge wall, dominated by QEMU slices and wakes, not the engine). LU caching waits for phase 7 and is dropped if the bench does not justify it. Event ceilings (`isolation_bridge` 32, `pulse_bridge` 64) are rate-independent and untouched because STEP stays a rate.

## 7. Determinism

The crossing is Δ = τ·ln((v∞ − v0)/(v∞ − v_th)), defined only when v_th lies strictly between v0 and v∞ (the (v0 − v_th)/(v∞ − v_th) form in the findings is ln of a negative for a 0→3.3 V step); the settle end is Δ_end = τ·ln(|v∞ − v0| / 1 µV). The log is **embsim's own** `ln_q`: fixed-point over the already-quantized 1 µV / 1 mΩ inputs (table + integer Newton, stated max error 1e-9 relative, no libm) — a reviewer is right that a platform `ln` inside an event instant is the first place a cross-OS ULP would change event *order* rather than a logged value, and the goldens are blessed on macOS and compared on the ubuntu-only leg (`ci.yml:60-66`). τ stays f64 from the quantized R_th and C (no τ_ns rounding: 25 Ω·100 pF is 2.5 ns, not 2 or 3); Δ is computed in f64 from `ln_q`'s result and rounded exactly once: t = t0 + round(Δ_ns). A Δ that rounds to 0 is **not scheduled** (round, not ceil), and the settle-end entry is armed only for nodes with an Analog sense — which is why a pad-scale RC on a 25 Ω driver into 10 pF leaves the bit grid untouched on every SPI edge. `Settling` is published only when the crossing rounds to ≥ 1 ns or an Analog sense exists; otherwise the DC projection is published at once. At t_cross the engine republishes with `level` flipped — a different tuple, so `same_state` delivers; at t_end it republishes the DC projection. `level_of(Settling)` is its `level` field (existing consumers stay correct; a reviewer is right that the draft's "evaluate at read time" could not deliver under `same_state`); `volts_at` is for RC-aware analog consumers. A capacitive node whose every source is released publishes `Analog(v0)` held (memory, no entries, no invented value). Same binary + same inputs → same bits (R12d); the macOS leg joins the determinism job for any fixture that carries a `Crossing` record before phase 5 blesses one. Every armed instant carries the cluster's solve generation and a wheel seq; it fires in (deadline, seq) order after same-instant drives (R11); a drive on a settling cluster re-solves with v0 = v(now), bumps the generation, and the stale entry is dropped on fire. PWL flips are one-at-a-time in declaration order from a cold start, bound 2N, so a pass is a function of (topology, drive table, t) alone — warm-starting from the previous solve made two histories reaching one drive table publish different states and broke `resolve_reference`; on exhaustion the element's nodes publish Floating with `Finding::NonConvergent`, never NaN, never a "last consistent solution" — every non-terminal node of the cluster, a path to an unmodelled rail notwithstanding (the rail's "up through the path" presentation is for a node nothing numeric reaches, not for a solve that failed). One cross-platform caveat, recorded before it can bite: an on-segment with `r_d` = 0 is stamped at the 1 µΩ floor (`g` = 1e6), so a reported branch current `g·(V_a − V_b) − i` is a catastrophic cancellation whose low bits are the elimination's rounding — deterministic per binary, but the first place an element-bearing golden blessed on macOS would diverge on ubuntu; no golden carries an element yet, and the phase that blesses the first one quantises published currents as the trace quantises states, or reports a vertical segment's current from KCL at its node. Sources stay slot → power → stuck; no HashMap on the path (D0). The incremental-vs-full test (`engine.rs:4877-4926`) is extended to terminals, Settling and PWL clusters as a hard gate. `rs422_determinism.rs` (`:88-121`, an open free-running divergence) is investigated in phase 0, before the sense→drive cascades that expose it multiply; until it is closed every new model's proving tests run in stepped mode only. Goldens: only four exist (`net_stuck_shared_node`, `nominal_analog_cluster`, `serial_levels`, `wake_ladder`); none carries Pulled ohms, caps or PWL, so every phase's gate is that all four pass **without re-blessing**. New fixtures (rc_step, i2c_bus) get goldens blessed once; CI still fails on a rewrite.

## 8. Phases (smallest first; each with its proving test)

0. **Census, baseline, gates.** `board/tests/cluster_census.rs` prints `topology.clusters[*].roots.len()` and the stub count for EC32MB/Edge/DS2; a `solve_bench` example records ns/solve for m ∈ {2, 4, 8, 11, 47}; the ROM boot counts escalated resolves (expect 0) and records edges/wall as baseline; the rs422 divergence gets a reproduction that fires on demand or a stepped-only rule. Proof: census committed as a fixture.
   *Done 2026-09-23; the figures every later phase is measured against.* Census, bare boards (`BuiltSystem::cluster_roots`, the then-existing `Board::stub_refs` + the registered facades named per board): **EC32MB** 83 clusters, largest 8 roots (`GND`, `Common_VDD`, `Common_LDOin`, the bucks' `SW`/`FB` nodes, the P59 pull-down net), stub count 23 (21 registered facades, the `P2Slot` placeholder counted as one, + `PCB`, `NC_Net`); **Edge** 209 clusters, largest 11 (`+3.3V`, nine LED anodes through their 220 Ω, the `D2` cathode through `L2`), stub count 50 (46 facades + H5–H8); **DS2** 22 clusters, largest 2, stub count 0. The 8 and 11 are the §6 offline sizings, confirmed. `solve_bench` (release, Apple M2): 0.72 / 1.2 / 2.6 / 3.7 / 46 µs per solve at m = 2 / 4 / 8 / 11 / 47 — one solve per edge at the 47-net collapse rule 1 prevents would cost four times today's whole per-edge budget, so the boundary rule is earned before phase 4 starts, and the LU cache stays unearned. (Phase 3's review pass re-measured it against the phase-2 tree, interleaved, on a machine six foreign processes held at 100 %: HEAD 1.25 / 2.00 / 4.4 / 6.6 / 75 µs against 1.33 / 2.13 / 4.4 / 6.7 / 76 µs — the absolute figures are that day's load, the ratios the record; the phase-3 review record has the tables.) ROM boot (`rom_boot_ec32mb`, asserted): 16 901 clock edges, 16 953 yields and publishes, **0 escalated solves**, 0.212 s wall, 12.5 µs/edge — the plan's 16 901 / 0.2 s, now a gate through `SystemHandle::escalated_solves`. The rs422 divergence fired at one run in twelve on the first `--ignored` invocation (0.2 s), a component's attach-time release interleaving with a sense delivery; the mechanism stays open and the decision is TESTING.md rule 9: every new model's proving test runs in stepped mode until it closes.
1. **One pipeline groundwork, value parser, rule 2, idle drives.** Classes `Mechanical`/`Probe`/`Switch`/`Pwl` as types; `Stubbed`/`Ignored`/`stub_refs`/`ExpansionHook` deleted from the registry and board build; `UnknownPart` names the value; `stub_count` becomes a never-rises gate; `PinDecl::idle`; the build fixed point and the build-equals-pre-wake invariant test. Proof: `"4.7uF 6.3V"` → 4.7e-6, `"47uH/3A"` → 47e-6, 66/66 EC32 caps parse; 15 kΩ slot High + 30 Ω slot Low on one root → `Driven(Low)`; 25 Ω vs 100 Ω → Analog(2.64) + finding; 25 vs 25 → Contention as today; a driver Low against `net_stuck` 3.3 V on its root → Analog(3.3) + finding; the 47 Ω edge still `Pulled(_, 47)` bit-identical; the unstuck-GND chain reads the winner's path, not 82 200; the two-sink wired-AND (either Low → `Driven(Low)`, both released → `Pulled(High, 4700)`); `PCB`/`NC_Net`/J701/J702/H5–H8 are `Mechanical` nodes; four goldens unchanged.
   *Declaration half done 2026-09-24* (the engine half — rule 2's projection and `Drive::Current` — is the next task). The registry has four classes beside `Registered` — `Switch { poles }`, `Pwl { spec, pins }` (spec a placeholder until phase 3), `Probe`, `Mechanical` — in one entry table with `register_switch`/`register_pwl`/`register_mechanical`; `Stubbed`, `Ignored`, `ExpansionHook`, `from_netlist_with_stubs`, `stub_refs` and both `STUB_REFS` lists are gone, and `RegistryError::UnknownPart` carries the value (on the EC32MB an unregistered `U100` reads `U100: … part "" with value "P2X8C4M64P"`). Every netlist part has a record and `Board::nodes()` reports `(reference, class)`. S301 is a four-pole `Switch` in both EC32MB registries (poles by `<n>_ON`/`<n>_OFF`, all open; position *n* is pole `n − 1`, `FLASH_SELECT_POLE`/`P59_PULL_UP_POLE`/`P59_PULL_DOWN_POLE` name the three the boot uses), J101 a one-pole switch open, SW1 a switch through the auto `SW_*` tier (pins 1/2), and `PCB`/`NC_Net`/`Mounting Hole Vss` register mechanical by value; a closed pole is an identity union honouring `pin_detach`, set by `Scenario::switch(ref, pole, state)` with `jumper` as its pole-0 form, and `rom_boot_ec32mb` now boots on `.switch` with no `pin_short` (16 901 edges, 0 escalations, unchanged). `PinDecl::idle: IdleDrive` (`KindDefault` | `Released` | `Thevenin`) is honoured in `add_pin_descriptor` wherever a declaration sets it, with the kind default kept where it does not, so no table moved; `PinDecl::{new, digital_in, digital_out, analog, power_in, power_out, passive, with_*}` are the constructors the stub helpers now delegate to. The build is a bounded fixed point (`BUILD_FIXED_POINT_BOUND = 8`): recorded attach drives are replayed, the changed states delivered to the senses recorded at attach, and the round repeated while a drive changes; past the bound `Finding::BuildNotSettled { passes, nets }`. Census after: **EC32MB** 83 clusters / largest 8 / stub count **20** (from 23: S301 a switch, `PCB`/`NC_Net` mechanical), **Edge** 209 / 11 / **45** (from 50: SW1 a switch, H5–H8 mechanical), **DS2** 22 / 2 / 0 — the never-rises fixture. Proof landed in `board/tests/one_pipeline.rs` (66/66 caps, 2/2 inductors, 15/15 resistors parse; the mechanical nodes; P59 `Pulled(High, _)` through pole 2 and `Pulled(Low, _)` through pole 3, floating with all open, unmerged with a detached contact; SPI_CS on P61 through the FLASH pole; `UnknownSwitchPole`; the value in the error) and `board/tests/build_fixed_point.rs` (a two-deep sense→drive chain rests in the build; the declared idle drive is the pin's state; a self-inverting ring reports `BuildNotSettled` at 8; **build snapshot == live pre-wake state on the EC32MB, the Edge board and the chain**). The four goldens pass without re-blessing.
   *Engine half done 2026-09-24.* Rule 2 is one function, `engine.rs` `project_root`, shared by the per-cluster pass and the test-only global reference: every Thevenin source with a resistive path to a root is a `ReachingSource` ranked by total ohms (slot impedance + the cached `dist` path; a terminal the path alone); total ≥ `WEAK_DRIVE_OHMS` (= `STREAM_COLLAPSE_THRESHOLD`, `net.rs`) is a pull and never contends; a source `ESCALATION_IMPEDANCE_RATIO` times the strongest or more loses (equal totals never lose — only ideal sources tie); an agreeing contest is the strongest's — `Driven` for a strong slot on the root, `Analog(v)` for a terminal on it, else `Pulled(level, path)` with a weak slot's own impedance in the path; a disagreeing contest solves the cluster once and projects through `V_IL`/`V_IH` (0.8/2.0 V, JESD8C.01, `net.rs`): inside → `Contention` + `AmbiguousLevel`, outside → `Analog(v)`, both with `Finding::Contention` naming the strong pins; pulls alone disagreeing (a divider) → `Analog(v)`, nothing reported. `levels_of`, the collapsed-resistance contention walk, escalation trigger 2, `edge_total_ohms`, `cluster_driver_level` are gone. `Drive { Thevenin, Current { amps } }` is the slot's type (`component.rs`), `PinHandle::drive`/`release` the entry, `set_drive` the alias; `normalise_drive` maps a non-finite impedance (or a non-finite current) to released at the slot; `Drive::Current` is `ClusterInjection` on the solve's right-hand side only, a cluster with a non-zero injection solves on request (an injection has no projection form), and an injection where no Thevenin source reaches is `Finding::CurrentIntoFloatingNode`. The event log writes a current drive as `<n>na`. The incremental-vs-full oracle's drive strategy gained 100 Ω, ∞ Ω and current drives. Two decisions the plan did not make, written down: **(a) operating-point precedence** — a cluster solved because an analog sense (or an injection) asked publishes the solved voltage on every root and raises no fight finding — including the two the old pass still reported under an analog sense, two disagreeing terminals on one root (a rail against a `net_stuck`) and two strong drivers disagreeing across less than 1 kΩ, which are silent there now and written down here as the narrowing they are — because `NetState::Contention` would hand the analog reader nothing; `nominal_analog_cluster` and `net_stuck_shared_node` both fight on the probed node (two ~25 Ω drivers; a driver against a stuck 0 V), so the "gains the finding it lacks" line above holds everywhere except under an analog sense until §10's `Sense { volts }` retires the precedence and those two goldens are re-blessed by that phase (*retired 2026-09-25 by §12 item 5's rules task, both goldens re-blessed there*); **(b) terminals are path barriers now** — `min_path_ohms` ends a path at a declared terminal (a `PowerOut`, modelled or NaN; a harness supply; a `net_stuck`) and never continues past one, which is rule 1 of the three in the reach matrix ahead of phase 4's cluster split: without it the ROM boot's MOSI pad, high through R303 to the stuck ground, ranked on `Common_VDD`'s feedback divider *beyond* ground as a pull within 10× of ground and bought a divider solve on each of the 24 MOSI edges. Proof landed in `engine::tests::source_strength` (16 cases: 15 kΩ High + 30 Ω Low → `Driven(Low)`, nothing reported, no solve; the lone 15 kΩ pad → `Pulled(High, 15000)`; 25 vs 100 → `Analog(2.64)` + one `Contention` naming both, one solve; 25 vs 25 → `Contention` + `AmbiguousLevel(1.65)`; a driver Low against `net_stuck` 3.3 V on its root → `Analog(3.3)` + the finding; the P58 shape, 25 on the root against 25 through 240 → each net `Driven` to its own pin's level, the fight named on both, no solve; the wired-AND → `Pulled(High, 4700)` / `Driven(Low)`; ∞ Ω → released, no solve, no table change; a NaN rail through 4.7 kΩ → `Pulled(High, 4700)`; 1 mA into 1 kΩ to ground → `Analog(1.0)`, one solve, and the same through `PinHandle::drive` on the live engine; an injection nothing reaches → `Floating` + `CurrentIntoFloatingNode` + `FloatingSense`; a source stops at a terminal; the analog precedence), the rewritten `competing_path…` (the driver's net takes the solver's value, the rail's net stays `Analog(3.3)`) and `disagreeing_drivers_across_a_small_resistor_solve_and_project_each_side_through_the_dead_band` (2.45 V / `Contention` at 0.85 V, both named; exactly 1 kΩ apart → two pulls, nothing reported), the probe `zz_probe_rail_vs_driver` promoted to `a_driver_through_a_small_resistor_outvotes_a_pull_to_the_opposite_rail` (`Pulled(level, 47)`), `a_driven_level_crosses_a_series_resistor` still `Pulled(_, 47)` bit-identical, `an_injected_short_outvotes…` still `Pulled(High, _)`, and on the EC32MB `one_pipeline::a_net_one_resistor_from_ground_reports_that_resistor`: `Net-(S301-4_OFF)` with ground over a `J203` finger reads `Pulled(Low, 10500)` — R303's own value, where the edge sum of its 8-root cluster is 82 200. Census unchanged (83/8/20, 209/11/45, 22/2/0; 0 solves at build). ROM boot: `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=0 wall=0.205s (12.11 us/edge)`. The four goldens pass without re-blessing.
   *Review pass 2026-09-24.* The build's sense log is held by the build alone and weakly by the inert link in every handle (a recorded callback captures its component's handles, so a strong link was a cycle that pinned every model's captured state — the flash image — for the life of the process; `build_fixed_point::the_build_releases_every_sense_callback_it_recorded`). The census fixture carries the exact mechanical set per board and a zero element count, and a fitted `Pwl` node with the placeholder spec is refused at assemble (`BoardError::UnmodelledElement`; `one_pipeline::a_declared_element_without_behaviour_builds_only_unpopulated`) — so neither `register_mechanical` nor `register_pwl` can lower the stub count without behaviour. An idle drive declared on a power or passive pin is refused at build (`BoardError::IdleOnSlotlessPin`, both routes). A `TestPoint*` on no net is mechanical, with more pins whatever the registry says of it, else the pin-count error; an explicit registration beats the two-pin `SW_*` guess. `validate_pins` names the first mismatch in declaration then netlist order. `Amps` is the current alias beside `Volts`/`Ohms`. The escalation counter is sequentially consistent.
2. **Switch, oscillator, gates, memory, P2 package, pads.** S301/SW1/JP1/J101 as switches; `Oscillator` + rate-through-capacitor routing; `LogicGate` for U101, U601 and the 21 SN74LVC1G14; `Psram`; `P2Package` with the XI sink replacing `with_crystal_hz`, `P2EdgeModule` and `P2Slot`; `pad_drive` + the `sensed()` fix in p2-qemu and p2core; ISO67xx promoted on the Edge; the p2iss fiat replaced by a `Pull`. Proof: `ec32mb_module` P59 `Pulled(High, 10500)` with pole 3 closed, `Pulled(Low, 10500)` with pole 4; SPI_CS `Driven(Low)` through pole 2; `rom_boot_ec32mb` boots with `.switch` and no `pin_short`; its clock from the rate on XI (HUBSET to 160 MHz from the delivered 20 MHz) — proven on the bench in `crystal_pll.rs`, and on the module once U402 is a rail (phase 4 inherits the item: the ROM never HUBSETs, so the boot could not observe it anyway); a pad at `{3.3 V, 15 kΩ}` with the net `Driven(Low)` reads 0 via `testp`, and the p2core differential stays 60 000/60 000; `one_pipeline` JP1 pads 1–2 put IC14.OUTC on SC_ENA, pads 2–3 put Q1's collector (KiCad `Jumper_3`: 1 = A, 2 = C the common, 3 = B); `wrpin(P_HIGH_15K)` on a released net → `Pulled(High, 15000)`, `P_HIGH_FLOAT` → released.
   *Models-and-boards half done 2026-09-24* (the P2 package — `P2Package`, the XI sink replacing `with_crystal_hz`, `pad_drive`, the `sensed()` fix in p2-qemu and p2core, the p2iss fiat — is the separate task after this one; nothing under `p2-qemu/` or in MaD's `SIL/p2core`, `SIL/p2iss`, `SIL/MaDSim` moved). What landed, and every decision the plan had not made, written down here as the smallest choice that let the work continue:
   - **`Oscillator`** (`models/src/oscillator.rs`, Seiko Epson TG2016SMN/TG2520SMN product brief): `parse_oscillator_hz` reads the `<n>M` / `<n>MHz` / `<n>.<n>M` token of the value (the module's `TG2520SMN 20.0000M-ECGNNM3` → 20 MHz, the datasheet's `26.000000MHz` standard form, nine cases); VCC sensed (`supply_up` at the datasheet's 1.7 V minimum); OUT a `PulseSource` publishing **one** rate at `t_str` = 1.0 ms max after the supply is seen up — a scheduled wake, never a publish at attach — and a held train when the supply drops. **OUT drives no DC level**: the datasheet characterises the clipped-sine output into a DC-cut capacitor (`Load_C` "DC cut capacitor = 0.01 µF") and names no DC level or source impedance, so inventing one would break rule 6; the net on the TCXO's side of `C132` reads floating at DC, which a cap-only net is. Registered for `X100` in both registries (`TCXO_PART`, from the value). The route is real: `Net-(X100-OUT)` → `C132` (1 nF) → `Net-(U101-2A)`, verified in `p2_ec32mb.net` (nets 20, 10, 11, 99).
   - **Rate routing through a capacitor** (`engine.rs` `route_pulses`; `Resolver::add_coupling` fed by `system.rs` from every two-pin capacitor with a parsed value, honouring `pin_detach`): a `PulseSink` reached only across one or more coupling capacitors is a `CoupledSink` carrying each crossing's capacitor, farads and far-node resistance estimate, and its own conduction segment as its gate. **The AC-coupling rule is judged at delivery, not at routing**: the rate is not known at build (routing runs before any train exists), so `pulse_update` and the once-at-registration delivery check `1/(2π·f·C) ≤ R_far / COUPLING_REACTANCE_RATIO` (`net.rs`, = `ESCALATION_IMPEDANCE_RATIO` = 10) per crossing and raise `Finding::PulseNotCoupled { net, capacitor, hz, reactance_ohms, far_ohms }` when it fails; the finding is live-only, and a held train (no rate) crosses unconditionally so a sink learns of a stop. **`R_far` is the smallest conduction edge incident to the far root, `+∞` when none** (a lone CMOS input) — not a Thevenin solve on the fast path, which rule 8 refuses and the plan's "smallest resistor edge" allowed; a strong driver on the far node is not in the estimate (that is a DC fight the node itself reports). **The coupled sink's delivery gate spans the far side's segment only and treats `Floating` as passable**: the capacitor is what sources the far node, the DC state of the source side cannot reach it, and `Contention` (a fought far node clamps the signal) is the only barrier — without this the U101 stage, whose input floats at DC until its own rate-mode drive biases it, could never receive the rate that biases it. Two sources reaching each other across a capacitor face each other (`StreamMismatch`) like two on one line. DC resolution is untouched: a capacitor is never a conduction edge (`one_pipeline`/`cluster_census` unchanged at 83/8, 209/11). On the module: `X_C(20 MHz, 1 nF)` = 7.96 Ω against `R101` = 100 kΩ → coupled.
   - **`LogicGate`** (`models/src/logic_gate.rs`; Nexperia 74LVC2G04 Rev. 13, TI SN74LVC1G14 SCES218AA): per channel the input is projected through the part's thresholds with **hold-last inside the band** (2G04: `V_IL` 0.8 / `V_IH` 2.0 V, Table 7; 1G14: `V_T−` **min** 0.84 V / `V_T+` **max** 1.87 V, §5.5 at `V_CC` = 3 V, the widest band any part is guaranteed not to have flipped in — the decision, since the table gives bounds and no typical); Y driven `t_pd` later as a scheduled wake (`t_pd` = the datasheet **maximum**, 4.1 ns → 5 ns and 4.6 ns → 5 ns on the integer wheel — the first integer instant at or after the bound, the conservative bound the isolators' impedance choice already set the precedent for; the review pass corrected 4.1 → 4, which rounded below it), high through `R_OH` = (3.0 − 2.3 V)/24 mA = 29.2 Ω and low through `R_OL` = 0.55 V/24 mA = 22.9 Ω (both datasheets' 24 mA rows); **an input with no level (floating, fought) releases Y** — neither datasheet says what an open input produces and the engine invents no level; **Y declares `IdleDrive::Released`** so a channel whose input has no level asks for nothing at attach (drive count 0). Rate mode: a train with a rate on A (a `PulseSink`) is relayed verbatim on Y (`PulseSource`) and Y is driven at `V_CC × 50 %` (the duty a train does not carry; the mid-point of the TCXO's 40–60 % symmetry) through `max(R_OH, R_OL)`, at once, the level sense ignored; a held train returns the channel to level mode. `V_CC` is the supply net's voltage, or the datasheet's 3.3 V typical-value supply when the engine has only a projection (the isolators' rule). Registered for `U101`/`U601` (`INVERTER_PART`, by-function pins) and the 21 `SN74LVC1G14DBV` (SOT-23 numbered pins) in both registries. At build the gates drive nothing (no wake has run), which is the honest pre-wake state; live, `U101` rests both stages at 1.65 V with **one drive and one relay per stage** and zero escalated solves.
   - **A source inside the dead band projects `Analog(volts)`** (`project_root`, rule 2 step 4, the one engine projection change): a slot source whose open-circuit voltage lies strictly between `V_IL` and `V_IH`, alone or the winner, has no level to project — a rate-mode gate's 1.65 V average would otherwise read `Driven(High)`, a lie a P2 `XI` sense would take for a stuck clock. On the root and through a resistor alike; nothing reported; no solve. `engine::tests::source_strength::a_source_inside_the_dead_band_projects_its_voltage`; the four goldens pass without re-blessing (none carries a mid-band single source).
   - **`Psram`** (`models/src/psram.rs`, AP Memory APS6404L-3SQR datasheet Rev. 2.3): on the shift engine factored out of the flash into **`models/src/spi_shift.rs` `ByteShifter`** (`SpiNorFlash` now rides it, public API and all its tests unchanged); CE# sensed; the SPI-mode column of §8.5 answered from an 8 MiB array — Read `03`, Fast Read `0B` (8 wait cycles), Write `02`, Read ID `9F` (24-bit dummy address, then `MF ID 0D`, `KGD 5D` PASS, then a six-byte EID that defaults to zeros because Figure 12 prints its fields and no value), Enter Quad `35`, Reset Enable `66` / Reset `99`, Wrap Boundary Toggle `C0` (recorded, not modelled); **SO is high-impedance until the first data bit** (Figures 6/9/12) and released on deselect (Figure 3), driven at the datasheet's 50 Ω; **QPI is out of scope** — after `35` the part answers nothing serially until a new part; no supply gate (as the flash); `t_CEM`, `t_PU`, page-crossing limits and Wrap 32 not modelled, a linear burst wraps the array. Registered for `PSRAM_PART` in both registries. `SIO2`/`SIO3` stay declared senses (unused in SPI mode, Table 2), so the module's floating-sense set is unchanged.
   - **ISO67xx promoted on the Edge** (`machine_parts::edge_registry`): `IC1`, `IC2`, `IC14`, `IC15`, `IC16` register `embsim_models::isolation::Iso67xx` from their part names, `IC14` with channel A (`INA` pin 3 on `P8` → `OUTA` pin 14) as the rate-carried STEP channel — the registration `isolation_bridge.rs` made per test, now the board's; that binary re-registers the same keys only to capture monitors. Consequence written down: with the module in its socket, `IC2` (the Raspberry-Pi block) sees its Pi side unpowered and drives its P2-side outputs at the ISO6742's default high (SLLSFJ6G Table 9-2, `VCCI` PD / `VCCO` PU), so socket fingers 61/62 (`EdgeBoard.P52`/`P53`) read `Driven(High)`; `machine_system::socket_pins_the_module_never_connects_stay_floating` became `…_get_nothing_from_it` and says so.
   - **Switches.** `JP1` (`Jumper_3_Open`) is a two-pole switch through the **auto tier**: a `Jumper_3_*` / `SolderJumper_3_*` symbol classifies as `Switch { poles: [1–2, 2–3] }` from the KiCad pinout (1 = A, 2 = C the common, 3 = B), each closed where the name says (`_Bridged12`, `_Bridged23`, `_Bridged123`), an explicit `register_switch` for the part name pairing the pads otherwise; `Classification::Jumper` lost its `selectable` field. On the Edge board pole 0 (pads 1–2) puts `IC14`'s `OUTC` on `SC_ENA`, pole 1 (pads 2–3) puts `Q1`'s collector there (`Net-(JP1-B)`), and the throws meet only when both are closed — the latent throw-to-throw short is gone. `J101` (one pole, open) and `SW1` (auto `SW_*`) were already switches from phase 1; verified in `one_pipeline`.
   - **`System::hold_time` / `SystemHandle::release_time`.** The TCXO arms its start-up wake at attach, so a live module runs past the build snapshot the instant time moves; `build_fixed_point::the_build_snapshot_is_the_live_systems_state_before_its_first_wake` now starts with time held, compares, then releases — "before its first wake" literally, the shape phase 4's `t_SS` rails will need too.
   - **Stubs deleted:** `boards/src/ec32mb.rs` lost `INVERTER_2G04_PINS`, `TCXO_PINS`, `SPI_FLASH_STUB_PINS` (dead: the live flash always replaced it), `PSRAM_PINS`; `machine_parts/mod.rs` lost `INVERTER_2G04_PINS`, `TCXO_PINS`, `PSRAM_PINS`, `ISO6742_PINS`, `ISO6741_PINS`, `ISO6740_PINS`, `ISO6721_PINS`, `SN74LVC1G14_PINS`. The test registry's `U301` is the live flash too — `machine_parts::ec32mb_registry` registers the blank 16 MiB part exactly as `boards` does and `w25q128jv.rs` re-registers the key with each case's own image; `SPI_FLASH_PINS`, whose data-out pin was declared an input so it could not contend, is gone (review pass); U401, U402/U403, U404, U501–U508, U1/U2/U3/Q1/IC3/IC4/IC6–IC13/U4–U8 stay until phases 3–4. **Census: EC32MB 83 / 8 / stub count 13** (from 20: X100, U101, U601, U302–U305), **Edge 209 / 11 / 19** (from 45: IC1, IC2, IC14, IC15, IC16 and the 21 inverters), **DS2 22 / 2 / 0**; mechanical sets and the zero element count unchanged.
   - **Proof landed:** `board/tests/oscillator_chain.rs` (stepped, own binary: with time held `XTAL_XI` floats and no rate is delivered; released, a probe on `XI` is delivered 20 MHz with `since_us` = 1000, `Net-(U101-2A)`, `Net-(U101-2Y)` and `XTAL_XI` read `Analog(1.65)`, both U101 stages in rate mode with one drive and one relay each, zero escalated solves, `XTAL_XO` still a floating sense; the coupling rule on a bench fixture: 10 pF at 20 MHz (796 Ω against 1 kΩ) → nothing delivered and `PulseNotCoupled { net: "B.IN", capacitor: "C1", hz: 20 000 000, reactance ≈ 795.77, far_ohms: 1000 }`, 100 nF → delivered, the node `Pulled(Low, 1000)` at DC either way); `board/tests/logic_gate_levels.rs` (an SN74LVC1G14's Y goes `Driven(Low)` exactly 5 ns after A went high, through 22.9 Ω, and high 5 ns after A went low, through 29.2 Ω, one drive per edge; a Schmitt input driven to 1.3 V (1G14) / 1.5 V (2G04) inside its band leaves Y untouched and the drop below the band flips it `t_pd` later — the Y log reads released, low, high and nothing else); `board/tests/psram_spi.rs` (a bit-banging master on `P56`/`P57`/`P52`/`P53` of the module reads `0D 5D` from `U302` over the nets, `P2_IO53` floating before select and after deselect, the other three PSRAMs' outputs floating throughout); `one_pipeline` (P59 `Pulled(High, 10 500)` through position 3 and `Pulled(Low, 10 500)` through position 4, exact; `SPI_CS` `Pulled(High, 10 500)` through the FLASH pole and `Driven(Low)` when a bench pin holds `P61` low; `JP1`'s four pole combinations by net merge); `ec32mb_module::the_clock_chain_rests_before_start_up_and_the_open_dip_switch_floats_its_strap` (the build snapshot: `XI`, `XO`, `P59` float); `registry::tests::a_three_pad_jumper_is_a_two_pole_switch`; `models` unit tests for the parser (9 cases), the start-up wake, the gate's projection and `t_pd`, the PSRAM command set and the shifter. The four goldens pass without re-blessing. **ROM boot** (`rom_boot_ec32mb`, with `X100`, `U101`, `U601` and the four PSRAMs live): `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=0 wall=0.212s (12.52 us/edge)` — the phase-0 baseline to the edge; the TCXO runs and `U101` drives `XTAL_XI` at 1.65 V one millisecond in, and the QEMU node, whose `XI` is still a plain sense, reads nothing from it until the P2 package task.
   - **What MaD will need** (files outside this repository, unedited): `SIL/p2iss/src/lib.rs` and `SIL/p2core/src/board.rs` — the P2 package task's `XI` `PulseSink` (the rate now on `XTAL_XI` is what replaces `with_crystal_hz`), `sensed()` = the net for weak or released pads, and the P59 fiat → a `Pull`; no MaD-side code references the deleted tables or `Classification::Jumper::selectable` (`SIL/MaDSim` registers only the ADS122U04 and the machine parts). A MaD system description that builds the EC32MB through `Ec32mb::registry()` gets the three models with it; one that declares a `Jumper_3_*` gets two poles and addresses them by index.
   *P2 package half done 2026-09-24* (`boards/src/p2.rs` `P2Package<Core>`, `p2-qemu` as a core, the pad strengths, the `sensed()` rule, the XI crystal; nothing in MaD's `SIL/p2core`, `SIL/p2iss`, `SIL/MaDSim` moved). What landed, and every decision the plan had not made, written down here as the smallest choice that let the work continue:
   - **`P2Package<C: P2Core>`** (`boards/src/p2.rs`; `boards` never depends on `p2-qemu`, `p2-qemu` now depends on `boards`): the 86-pin facade the netlist gives `U100` — 64 pads `DigitalBidir` with `IdleDrive::Released`, `VDD`/`GND`/the 16 `VIO` `PowerIn`, `RESN`/`TEST` `DigitalIn`, `XI` `DigitalIn` + `PulseSink`, `XO` `DigitalOut` released — around a core. The trait is two methods, `attach(&mut self, pads: P2Pads)` and `start`; `P2Pads` is the node interface narrowed to the pads: `pad(pin)` (a `PinHandle`), `on_pad_sense(pin, cb)`, `on_crystal(cb: Fn(Option<u64>))`, `on_reset(cb: Fn(P2ResetState))`, `on_wake_ns`, `schedule_at_ns`. The package installs its own `XI` pulse sink and `RESN`/`VDD` senses **after** the core has attached, freezes the core's subscriptions into shared slices, and delivers with no lock held; `P2PackageHandle::{crystal_hz, reset}` reads what it last delivered. `P2Package::held_in_reset()` (core `HeldInReset`, subscribes to nothing) replaced `boards/tests/ec32mb.rs`'s `P2Slot` and `machine_parts`' copy; `P2Package::native(McuComponent)` (`impl P2Core for McuComponent` hands it the package's whole net I/O — it finds `P0`/`P2` by name and bridges as on a board of its own; the narrowing to the pads is by what the core names, said in its doc, not enforced by a filter) replaced `P2EdgeModule`, now a type alias with `p2_edge_module(name)`; `p2x8c4m64p_pins`/`pin_name` moved to `embsim_boards::p2` and are re-exported by `p2-qemu`. Every P2 test passes on the package.
   - **`P2ResetState { resn, vdd }`** is the engine's own projection (`level_of`) of the two pins, `out_of_reset()` = `RESN` `High` ∧ `VDD` `High`; no voltage window is applied, since the datasheet's `VDD` range is the START gate's to cite (phase 4). The QEMU core records it (`P2QemuHandle::reset`) and does not act on it yet — the START gate lands with the rails that make `VDD` read a voltage.
   - **Pads are not senses until a core reads them.** `DigitalBidir` registers no digital sense of its own, so the `FloatingSense` findings the `DigitalIn` facades produced on every unconnected pad are gone; a `DigitalBidir` pin declared `IdleDrive::Released` that a component subscribes to through `on_sense` **is** a digital sense from that subscription on — the pad is read, and a floating one is `Finding::FloatingSense` (review pass 2026-09-24: `PreparedPin::reads_when_released` rides on the `PinHandle`; the build fixed point declares the sense to the resolver and re-resolves; live, `on_sense` sends `Command::DeclareRead` ahead of its `RegisterSense`, and the engine queues the declarations and applies them together after the drain batch with one full pass — applying each as it arrived left the topology cache stale for every attach-time drive behind it in the batch, a full resolution and a rebuild each, which read as 0.38 s on the ROM boot against its 0.21 s baseline before the batching). `HeldInReset` subscribes to nothing, so the shipped module's bare pads float with no finding; the QEMU core subscribes to all 64 pads — it cannot know ahead which the guest will sample — so its floating pads are reported, as the facades' were; the native core subscribes to the pads its HAL tables bridge. On the module the P59 strap was never unreported: the boot flash's `DI_IO0` is a declared input on that net, so `Finding::FloatingSense` on `P2_IO59` stands whatever fills the slot (`one_pipeline`'s two P59 cases assert it again, attributed to the flash; the reviewer's "nothing is reported" was wrong on this board). The gap the rule closes is a pad on a net nothing else reads — `P0`, on a bare card-edge finger: `one_pipeline::a_pad_is_a_floating_input_only_to_the_core_that_samples_it` puts a core that reads P0 in the slot and asserts the finding, a package held in reset and asserts its absence, and a core that reads P59 with the pull-down closed and asserts `Pulled(Low, 10 500)` with no finding. `ec32mb_module::the_clock_chain_rests…` still asserts the strap's `Floating` state. `XO` is a released output, so `XTAL_XO` floats with no finding (`oscillator_chain` and `ec32mb_module` updated). `XTAL_XI` stays a reported floating sense at build.
   - **Pad strengths** (`embsim_boards::p2::{pad_modes, pad_drive, PadMode, PadDrive}`, Parallax Propeller 2 Silicon Documentation v35, "Smart Pins" → "Pin Configuration Modes", the `%0000_CIO_HHH_LLL` word): bits 13:11 (`OUT` = 1) / 10:8 (`OUT` = 0) → fast = `P2_FAST_OHMS` (a named 25 Ω placeholder with a provenance TODO naming the datasheet's DC characteristics to measure), 1K5/15K/150K → `P2_PULL_{1K5,15K,150K}_OHMS`, FLOAT → released; the current-source modes → `PadDrive::CurrentSource(mode)`, which the QEMU core treats as released and logs **once per process** (`Drive::Current` is the encoding they take when a caller exists). **A word whose mode selector (bits 20:17) is not `%0000`** (DAC/ADC/comparator) reads as fast both ways — today's behaviour, the smallest claim about modes not modelled. **A pad drives high at `LOGIC_HIGH_VOLTS`** (3.3 V nominal) until the bank rails are modelled and the package can hand each pad its `VIO`. `Bus::published` is `[Option<Option<TheveninDrive>>; 64]`; `wrpin` on a pad whose `DIR` is set routes through `recompute_and_mark` — stamped at the **executing cog's** clock via `p2host_current_cog()`, which `hostdrive.c` already exported, so the plan's "stamped at `machine_now_ns()` until the vtable carries a cog" caveat never applied — and the wake republishes the pad; a mode write that leaves the drive unchanged is no pad change (a float-mode `drvh` publishes nothing).
   - **`sensed()`**: `(strong & out) | (!strong & in_ext)` where `strong` is the pads whose **published** drive is under `WEAK_DRIVE_OHMS`, recomputed at each publish — a released pad and a pad pulling through a resistive mode both read their net, a fast pad its own `OUT` bit. `pad_modes.rs` runs a hand-assembled guest (`wrpin ##P_HIGH_15K, #0` / `drvh #0` / `testp #0 wc` / `wypin`, checked against flexspin's listing) against a bench sink: the pull-up reads **0** through `testp`, `P2.P0` is `Driven(Low)` with no `Contention`, `P2.P1` (the same word on a net of its own) `Pulled(High, 15000)`, `P2.P2` (`P_HIGH_FLOAT`) `Floating`, 2 publishes, 0 escalations. The p2core differential is unchanged: 60 000 / 60 000 states identical (`compared 60000, identical`).
   - **The crystal is the rate on `XI`.** `with_crystal_hz` and `DEFAULT_CRYSTAL_HZ` are gone; `crystal_of(train)` = `Some(freq_hz)` for a train with a rate, `None` for a held one; the core drains it at each wake and `clock_hz(mode, crystal: Option<u64>) -> Option<u64>` derives the PLL from it. **No crystal, no clock**: a guest that selects a crystal-derived clock while nothing reaches `XI` **stalls** — the wake runs no slice and arms nothing — and the crystal's arrival (`on_crystal` on the engine thread) re-arms it; the new segment starts at the machine's current clocks with `from_ns` = that wake's instant, so the guest resumes at the instant its clock existed and no frequency is invented. A crystal that changes under a crystal-derived clock re-segments at the machine's current clocks. `crystal_pll.rs` proves both: a bench clock on `P2.XI` at 5 ms; the guest waits 1 ms, `HUBSET`s `$010007F0` then `$010007F3` (flexspin's 160 MHz word), and four back-to-back pad writes land **after 5 ms**, 12–13 ns apart (one two-clock instruction at 160 MHz on the integer grid), with `crystal_hz()` = 20 MHz on the node and the package; the unit tests hold `clock_hz(PLL, Some(20 MHz))` = 160 MHz and `clock_hz(PLL, None)` = `None`.
   - **The ROM boot's crystal is `None`, on purpose, until phase 4.** The module's TCXO runs from `Common_VDD`, the 1.8 V core rail, and `U402` is a facade whose `SW` is a NaN `PowerOut`; with the bench ground stuck, `Common_VDD` projects `Pulled(Low, 23800)` through the feedback divider and the TCXO never starts (`oscillator_chain` powers the module without a stuck ground and the rate reaches the package: `P2PackageHandle::crystal_hz()` = 20 MHz, now asserted there). Holding `Common_VDD` at 1.8 V from the bench was **measured and refused**: a second real terminal across the divider inside the ground cluster re-solves it on every P59 edge — 30 escalated solves against the 0 the boot is held to, exactly the cluster split phase 4's terminal boundaries remove. A bench clock on `XTAL_XI` is refused too: `U101.1Y` is a pulse source on that net, and two sources facing each other are `StreamMismatch`. So `rom_boot_ec32mb` boots with `P2Package::new(P2Qemu)` as `U100`, asserts the **cause** — `Common_VDD` reads `Pulled(Low, 23800)` through the divider, the TCXO's supply below its 1.7 V minimum — and beside it the consequence, `crystal_hz()` = `None` on the node and the package, tagged for phase 4: the day the rail is real the cause assertion fails first and the crystal assertion flips to `Some(20 MHz)`. The XI → `HUBSET` → PLL path is proven on the bench.
   - **`P2Package::held_in_reset` is a modelled node** in the census (the package's declarations and senses are behaviour; a core in reset is a state): **EC32MB 83 / 8 / stub count 12** (from 13: `U100`), **Edge 209 / 11 / 19**, **DS2 22 / 2 / 0**; mechanical sets and the zero element count unchanged. `EC32MB_MODELLED` names `U100`.
   - **Proof landed:** `board/tests/p2_package.rs` (stepped, own binary: a bench push-pull clock on `XI` → the package reports 20 MHz, a held train → `None` — a bench source must carry a DC level for the route to be signal-capable, the capacitor-coupled TCXO being the case where `Floating` is passable; `RESN`/`VDD` from bench supplies → `P2ResetState` by the engine's level rule, `out_of_reset` only with both; every pad of a package in reset `Floating` and a bench sink on one `Driven(Low)` with no `Contention`; the declaration itself); `boards/src/p2.rs` unit tests (the facade, 13 `WRPIN` words → their Thevenins, `DIR` clear → released, the Spin2 constants' fields, `crystal_of`, `out_of_reset`); `p2-qemu` unit tests (the fast-pad/net read, the pulling pad reading its net, a mode change republishing at the new strength, the current-source mode presenting nothing, `clock_hz` with and without a crystal, the delivered rate drained into the PLL arithmetic); `pad_modes.rs`, `crystal_pll.rs` and `rom_boot_ec32mb.rs` above; `boards/tests/ec32mb.rs::the_package_declares_the_netlists_u100_pins`. **ROM boot:** `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=0` — the phase-0 baseline to the edge, 60 000 / 60 000 states identical to p2core. The four goldens pass without re-blessing.
   - **What MaD will need** (unedited): `SIL/p2core/src/board.rs:482-484` `sensed()` takes the strong-mask rule (a pad whose *published* drive is under `WEAK_DRIVE_OHMS` reads `OUT`, every other pad reads the net) and a `pad_drive` from the `WRPIN` word — `p2core` may not depend on embsim (its `Cargo.toml` says so), so the decoding is replicated or `embsim_boards::p2::pad_drive` is taken by the `p2iss` side that publishes; `SIL/p2iss/src/lib.rs` implements `P2Core` for the ISS node (`attach(P2Pads)`: pads from `pads.pad(n)`, senses from `on_pad_sense`, the crystal from `on_crystal` in place of any constant, the reset state from `on_reset`), its harness tests fill `U100` with `P2Package::new(iss)`, and the P59 fiat at `lib.rs:946-951` (`bus.inner.set_input_level(59, true)`) goes — `rom_boot_net.rs`/`rom_serial_net.rs` gain a `Pull` on P59 (a resistor to the `VIO` rail, the strap the ROM samples) since their harnesses have a P61 strap and a MISO pull-up and no P59 source, and the EC32 path uses S301 pole 3; tests there that asserted `FloatingSense`/`DigitalIn` on pads assert net state, or the finding under a core that subscribes to the pad (the ISS node subscribes to what it reads, so a floating pad it reads is reported), as here. `SIL/MaDSim` registers only the ADS122U04 and the machine parts and is unaffected; nothing in MaD imported `p2x8c4m64p_pins`, `pin_name`, `with_crystal_hz` or `DEFAULT_CRYSTAL_HZ`.
   *Review pass 2026-09-24.* **A terminal is a barrier to rate routing as it is to projection**: `route_pulses` passes the declared terminal roots (`power_sources` + `stuck_sources`, by root — the same list `resolve` builds) to both reaches, the conduction `dist` and the capacitor walk, and neither relaxes out of one (the source's own root excepted, as `min_path_ohms` already had it), so a rate coupled into a stuck ground or a rail is shunted there and never forwarded through the next decoupling capacitor, and two sources that merely share decoupling to one terminal do not face each other; the walk records the far end at the crossing — `(capacitor, far root)` on the path — so `far_end_of`'s depth reconstruction and its tie to the near end are gone. Proof: `oscillator_chain::a_terminal_shunts_a_rate_coupled_into_it` (source → 100 nF → stuck ground → 100 nF → a 1 kΩ-biased sink: nothing delivered, no finding) and `two_sources_decoupled_to_one_terminal_do_not_face_each_other` (each 100 nF to the stuck ground: no `StreamMismatch`, the sink on one source's own net still delivered its rate).
