Nothing on the three boards moved (no pulse-carrying net on them has a shunt capacitor; census 83/8/12, 209/11/19, 22/2/0 and the ROM boot's 16 901 / 0 unchanged). **The coupling ratio's derivation, written down**: the physical bound for a series capacitor into a resistive node is the −3 dB point, `X_C ≤ R_far` (ratio 1); `COUPLING_REACTANCE_RATIO` = 10 is embsim's conservative constant, borrowed from the strength ranking and said so in `net.rs`, and the refused 10 pF / 1 kΩ / 20 MHz case (|H| ≈ 0.78, a 2.6 V swing a Schmitt input would pass) is refused by the margin, not by physics — §12 item 6 (capacitors) may derive the margin from the receiver's declared thresholds (pass fraction ≥ hysteresis / V_pp). `Finding::PulseNotCoupled` is deduped per distinct occurrence by `report_finding` like every live finding (a re-segmenting train at one rate raises it once; a rate that changes is a new verdict), so no dedup set was added. **A stall consumes the yield it shares a slice with**: the QEMU node's `wake` takes `p2host_take_yield()` before its stalled return, so the pad change a guest makes in the same slice as the clock-selecting `HUBSET` is published at the resume instant by the pending branch and never fires again as a phantom pad change; `crystal_pll.rs` holds `yields()` = 4 for its four pad writes. **`System::hold_time` holds in both pacing modes** — a reviewer read the free-running mode as ignoring it, but virtual time is only the counter the engine advances (`virtual_clock`: "never wall time"; `is_stepped()` is always true, there is one loop) and the release gate sits before its only advance; the docs now say so. The ROM-boot differential is reproducible from this tree: `p2-qemu/README.md` "The state trace" carries the exact commands (the flash image, the `p2state` reference, the extraction and the comparison from `romtest.sh`) and the run is recorded there. **The flash releases DO while deselected**: registering the live `U301` in the test registry put `P2_IO58` `Driven` on the bare module (`ec32mb_module::the_bridged_tx_pin_is_the_only_driver`), because `SpiNorFlashComponent::publish_do` drove DO whenever an array was fitted, with `miso()`'s "a released line idles high" standing in for a pull-up the netlist does not name (rule 6); the datasheet says the pin is at high impedance while `/CS` is high (W25Q128JV Rev F §4.1 "Chip Select (/CS)", p.9), so `publish_do` now drives only while selected (`SpiNorFlash::selected`), DO declares `IdleDrive::Released`, and `w25q128jv::the_data_line_floats_while_the_part_is_deselected` holds it on a bench with no pull-up; the ROM boot is unchanged (the ROM reads DO only with the part selected). The U301, `t_pd`, JP1, proof-clause, `McuComponent` and cause-beside-consequence corrections are in the bullets above.

3. **PWL elements + branch current.** Diodes, LEDs, CCRs, FETs with body diodes, the BJT, the opto LED branches; `GMIN`; cold start. Proof: Edge D3 lit with I ≈ (3.3 − Vf)/220 when its inverter output is **high**, anode ≈ 0 V when low; U3/U401 conduct forward (body diode on → V_gs < V_th → channel on → diode off, the start-up sequence) and block reversed, without `pin_short`; Q1 base Analog(0.65–0.75) and SC_ENA's voltage with the opto load; end-switch loop regulated at 10 mA ± 1 %, not 27 mA; non-convergence fixture → `NonConvergent`, never NaN; two histories reaching one drive table publish identical states; incremental == full for PWL.
   *Solver half done 2026-09-24* (the parts on the boards — `D1`/`D2` and the 21 LEDs from the library, `U3`/`U401` with their body diodes in place of the `pin_short` stand-ins, `Q1`, the eight CCRs, the opto LED branches, and the retirement of the `nsi50010`/`vo2631` chain trick with its 6.92 Ω / 100 Ω tunings — are the separate task after this one; no board registry loads the library, so the census is unchanged at **EC32MB 83 / 8 / 12, Edge 209 / 11 / 19, DS2 22 / 2 / 0** with zero elements on all three, and the mechanical sets as before). What landed, and every decision the plan had not made, written down as the smallest choice that let the work continue:
   - **Declaration** (`component.rs`, §11 as written): `PwlCurve::{Diode { vf, r_d }, Channel { r_on }}`, `RegionTest::{AtLeast(v), AtMost(v)}` on `V(control) − V(b)` (an N-channel's positive `V_GS(th)`, a P-channel's negative one), `Branch { a, b, curve, control }`, `Component::branches()` defaulting to none; a branch naming a pin the facade does not declare is `PinFacadeMismatch` at `Board::from_netlist`, for registered components and specs alike. **A channel with no control is a declared-open switch — always off** — the smallest reading of `control: Option`, and what a detached control pin becomes, rather than a fourth build error. The registry's `PwlSpec` is real: `{ pins, branches: Vec<PwlBranch> }` with `PwlSpec::diode(anode, cathode, vf, r_d)`, `with_branch`, `with_controlled_branch`; `register_pwl(key, spec)`; `PartClass::Pwl { spec }` and `Registered { pins, branches }`; `BoardError::UnmodelledElement` is gone with the placeholder it refused.
   - **The library** (`models/src/pwl_library.rs`, provenance per constant): **SS36** — Vishay SS32 thru SS36, Document Number 88751, Rev. 04-Aug-15, Electrical Characteristics: `V_F` maximum instantaneous forward voltage **0.75 V** at `I_F` = 3.0 A (the SS35–SS36 column; the SS32–SS34 column is 0.50 V, and the task's "0.45 V?" matches neither), `I_F(AV)` 3.0 A — keyed `SS36-E3/57T` (the EdgeBoard export's number) and `SS36` (its value); **LTST-C190KGKT** — Lite-On BNS-OD-C131/A4, Electrical Optical Characteristics p. 3 of 9: `V_F` **2.0 V typical**, 2.4 V maximum at `I_F` = 20 mA, AlInGaP green, continuous `I_F` 30 mA (Absolute Maximum Ratings p. 2) — keyed on its number, the value being the bare `LED`. **Both on-segments are vertical (`r_d` = 0)**: each datasheet tabulates one `V_F` point and a slope needs two, so the modelled drop is the tabulated one at any current — an upper bound below the test current (the Vishay Fig. 3 and Lite-On Fig. 2 curves say so, and a number read off a curve is not a citation). No generic "red LED" entry: nothing cites one. Keying works through `ComponentDecl::mpn` (the netlist parser now reads `Manufacturer_Part_Number` / `MPN`, property or field, case-insensitively) and the registry's lookup order **part name → manufacturer part number → value**. **The `LED`/`D_*` auto tier yields to an explicit entry and stays the DC-open passive without one** (`registry.rs`, tier 1e): the symbol is a guess that the part is open, an entry is a statement about the purchasable part — so the boards' diodes classify exactly as today until the parts task registers them, and whether an LED with no entry is then `UnknownPart` was left to that task (decided in the review pass: it is); a diode whose symbol the auto tier does not know and whose number and value the library lacks is `UnknownPart` now (`pwl_elements::a_diode_the_library_does_not_hold_is_an_unknown_part`).
   - **Stamping** (`cluster.rs`): `GMIN_OHMS` = 1 GΩ (the plan's reason, on the constant), `PWL_SOLVES_PER_ELEMENT` = 2; `ClusterInputs { sources, injections, terminals, elements }` — **no regions**; the flip loop cold-starts every element off, evaluates the tests in declaration order, flips the **first** that disagrees, bound `2N` linear solves; `ClusterSolution { node_states, regions, branch_currents: Vec<Option<Amps>>, converged, solves, unknowns }`. Off = the leakage conductance both ways; a diode on = `V_f` Norton pair + `1/r_d` (a 0 Ω segment at the ideal floor); a channel on = `1/r_on`; a test on a node no source reaches is off. **Leakage-only** was first the true `R_th` — each unknown's Thevenin resistance from `G·x = e_i` on the final matrix, an elimination per unknown, `R_th ≥ 100 MΩ` (`LEAKAGE_ONLY_OHMS`) publishing Floating — and the review pass replaced it with the structural test the documented semantics name ("a node only leakage reaches"): a reach from the sources and terminals over the resistor edges and the stamps that are more than the bare leakage (a conducting region, a regulator's ohmic segment, a current source), `O(m + edges)`, unreached nodes Floating however many gigaohms meet there; `LEAKAGE_ONLY_OHMS` is gone with the probe (see the review record for why). **Non-convergence floats every non-terminal node of the cluster**, not only the elements' own: in a conduction cluster every node is coupled to its elements, and a partial "last consistent" set is what the plan refuses; terminals keep their constants, currents are `None`, regions read all off. The trait stays `&self`.
   - **Terminals as constants (item 4)**: Dirichlet elimination for a supernode exactly one terminal voltage holds — the node leaves the unknowns and every edge or element touching it stamps `g·V_T` onto the neighbour's right-hand side; a supernode two terminals disagree on keeps the Norton fight at the floor, so `stuck_fault_fighting_a_power_rail_projects_contention` is bit-identical. **Confined to clusters that carry an element**, and here is the measurement: applied to every cluster it passed every unit test and the `nominal_analog_cluster` golden, and failed `net_stuck_shared_node` — not by a microvolt but by 48 records (17 against 65): with the stuck node held at exactly 0 V, the per-drive nanovolt re-publications of that node the trace records (the Norton floor's drop under each new drive, `0uv` once quantized, a new state bit-for-bit each time) are gone. The gate is the four goldens without re-blessing, so the resolver hands terminals over as `ClusterTerminal`s only for element clusters and as the 0 Ω sources they always were for linear ones; phase 4, which makes every terminal its own cluster, changes the linear clusters' hands and re-blesses the two analog goldens (`cluster.rs` module docs, "Terminals are constants", carries the same record). What it buys now: the LED chain solves at **m = 2** — `engine::tests::elements::an_led_chain_to_a_terminal_solves_with_two_unknowns` hands a spy solver the rail as a constant, reads `unknowns = 2`, `Y = 3.3 − 25·I`, anode 2.0 V, ground exactly 0 V, one escalation.
   - **Engine** (`engine.rs`): an element is a **membership edge** (`a`–`b`, and `control`–`b`, so the gate is in-cluster) in `build_topology`, never a path for rule 2's ranking; `ClusterTopo.elements` in declaration order; a cluster with elements is `on_request` — it solves whenever dirty, once per pass, cold every time (no warm start: `pwl_elements::two_histories_reaching_one_drive_table_publish_identical_states`). Its states are the solve's on every root — the far side of a diode has no resistive path for the ranking to see — and **rule 2's fights among the linear sources are still reported** (`project_root` fed the solved voltage; decision: unlike an analog-sense cluster, an element cluster raises `Contention`/`AmbiguousLevel` beside its `Analog(v)`, since the reader is handed the operating point either way). `Finding::NonConvergent { cluster, elements, solves }` — `solves` added to the plan's two fields as the observable of the bound (the fixture asserts `2N` through it). The test-only reference resolver mirrors all of it, and the incremental-vs-full oracle's random boards now carry diodes and channels with random controls (2000 cases). **Currents**: every solved cluster yields the current into each endpoint (`(V − V_oc)/Z`, `−I` for an injection, 0 for a released slot) and through each element, published beside the states as a `CurrentTable`; `PinHandle::sense_current() -> Option<Amps>` (decision: `Option`, the `Sense { volts: Option }` shape, in place of the plan's bare `Amps` — `None` says no solve produced one); `ComponentNetIo::on_branch(pin, Fn(Option<Amps>))` sends `DeclareRead { kind: Instrument }` ahead of `RegisterCurrent` — the instrument **escalates its cluster and nothing else** (first written as an analog-sense declaration, which the review pass found reporting every open opto loop on the Edge board as a floating input; see the review record) — delivered once at registration and on change, on the build path recorded and re-delivered by the fixed point like a sense; refused at attach on a pin with no drive slot and no branch (a terminal, a power pin, a passive pin of nothing). `BuiltSystem` and `SystemHandle` gain `branch_current("Board.Ref")` (the one branch of a two-terminal element) and `pin_current("Board.Ref.Pin")` (the same sum by path). The build stamps elements and reads currents too: build snapshot == live pre-wake state on the channel bench.
   - **Proof landed**: `board/tests/pwl_elements.rs` (stepped, own binary, twelve cases): the library diode forward — anode 0.75 V, `I = (3.3 − 0.75)/220` within 1 %, `pin_current` +I at the anode and −I at the cathode — and reversed — anode at its terminal, under 10 nA; a switched channel on/off/on for an N-type (`AtLeast(2)`) and a P-type (`AtMost(−2)`) test; two channels chasing each other → `NonConvergent { cluster: "B.VDD", elements: ["B.Q1", "B.Q2"], solves: 4 }` at build and live, both nodes Floating, terminals at their constants, no NaN, no current; the toggled and the untoggled bench bit-identical on every net and current; a leakage-only cathode Floating with `branch_current` 0; the instrument on a Low sink under a 1 kΩ pull-up reading `3.3/1025` A to a nanoamp, equal to `(3.3 − v)/1000` for the published `v`, delivered at registration and again as 0 on release, the cluster escalated; the instrument refused on a `PowerIn` pin; build == live for the element cluster; the unknown diode refused by name while a `D_*` symbol with no entry stays the open passive. `cluster.rs` unit tests (nine, on `ClusterInputs` directly): the two-unknown LED chain, the fought terminal at the mid-value, forward in two solves / reversed in one, the channel matrix, non-convergence at exactly `2N` = 4, leakage-only, the uncontrolled channel. `engine.rs`: the m = 2 chain, the off diode's far side in-cluster and floating, the oracle with elements. `registry.rs`/`netlist.rs`/`board.rs`/`pwl_library.rs` unit tests: the lookup order, the yield, the number parsed from both export forms, branch pins validated, every library entry a diode from pin `2` to pin `1`. `one_pipeline::an_element_registered_by_specification_is_a_node_when_fitted` replaces the placeholder test (the ledger shows `pipeline.element-declared-ahead-of-behaviour` removed and `pipeline.element-registered-by-spec` added: the behaviour it claimed — a fitted element refused — is the one this phase retires). Census unchanged; the four goldens pass without re-blessing. **ROM boot** (`rom_boot_ec32mb`): `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=0`. The wall figure this record first carried ("the same band" as the stashed HEAD) was wrong: a back-to-back A/B by the reviewers read HEAD 0.221–0.226 s against 0.238–0.241 s for this tree (13.1–13.4 against 14.1–14.3 µs/edge, +7 %), the per-pass cost of a current table copied on every pass, an endpoint entry pushed for every slot of every dirty cluster and an instrument walk after every publish — paid by a boot that never solves and subscribes nothing. The review pass made all of it free when nothing solved (the review record has the A/B after).
   - **What MaD will need** (unedited): `ComponentDecl` gained `mpn: Option<String>` — a MaD-side literal constructor of it, if one exists, needs the field (`SIL/MaDSim` registers only the ADS122U04 and the machine parts and constructs none as far as this repository shows); `PartClass::Registered` gained `branches` and `Pwl` lost its `pins` (patterns with `..` are unaffected); `register_pwl` takes `(key, PwlSpec)`; a MaD registry that wants the boards' diodes calls `embsim_models::pwl_library::register`.
   *Parts half done 2026-09-24.* Every nonlinear part on the EC32MB and the Edge board is an element, and the stand-ins are gone: `module_polarity_fet_conducting` / `edge_polarity_fet_conducting` (the `pin_short`s for `U401`/`U3`) and every test that used them; the `nsi50010`, `vo2631` and `npn_switch` models with the "chain, not a mesh" rule and the 6.92 Ω / 100 Ω tunings; the stub tables `POLARITY_FET_PINS`, `NSI50010_PINS`, `OPTO_6N137_PINS`, `VO2631_PINS`, `APM4953_PINS`, `NPN_PINS`. What landed, and every decision the plan had not made, written down as the smallest choice that let the work continue:
   - **The library** (`models/src/pwl_library.rs`, every number cited beside its constant; the bound that guarantees the region an entry names, the typical recorded beside it): **APM4953** (ANPEC datasheet, Electrical Characteristics: `V_GS(th)` −1/−1.5/−2 V → the channel is on at `V_GS` ≤ −2 V, the bound every part has turned on beyond; `R_DS(on)` 60 mΩ max at −10 V, the Edge's −12 V gate drive; body diode `V_SD` 1.3 V max at 1.7 A; keys `APM4953KC-TRG`, `APM4953`), declared as **the placed half** — pins `1` S1, `2` G1, `7`/`8` D1 — because a KiCad multi-unit export carries only the placed unit's nodes and a facade names the netlist's pins exactly (the second half is a second entry the day a board places it); **Si3417DV** (Vishay 62890 Rev. A: `V_GS(th)` −1/−3 V → on at ≤ −3 V; `R_DS(on)` 36 mΩ max at −4.5 V, the module's −5 V drive; `V_SD` 1.2 V max at 5.8 A; keys `SI3417DV-T1-GE3` (the module's `MPN` field), `SI3417DV`; pins `D`/`G`/`S` as the transcription names them); both FETs are the **body diode drain→source first, then the channel** gated `AtMost(−V_th)`, so the start-up reads in declaration order — diode on, channel on, diode off — in exactly `2N` = 4 solves under a load (`cluster::tests::a_polarity_fet_starts_through_its_body_diode_and_ends_on_its_channel`) and 2 with none; **NSI50010Y** (onsemi NSI50010Y/D Rev. 3: `I_reg(SS)` 10 mA typ at 7.5 V; `V_overhead` 1.8 V typ at 80 % — the knee: below it the ohmic segment `1.8 V / 10 mA` = 180 Ω, at or above it a 10 mA source; stated: 100 % at the knee where the part gives 80 %, 2.8 mA at 0.5 V where the Features line gives 4 mA — the curve is between the segments; reverse bias conducts through the segment, `V_R` 500 mV rating noted; key `NSI50010YT1G`, the MPN and value — the symbol's `NSI50010YT1G_1` is not a key); **2N3904/MMBT3904** (onsemi 2N3903/D Rev. 9: `V_BE(sat)` 0.65 V min at 10 mA/1 mA the knee; `h_FE` 100 min at 10 mA; `V_CE(sat)` 0.2 V max at 10 mA → `r_sat` 20 Ω; keys `MMBT3904-TP`, `2N3904`, `MMBT3904`; pins `1` E, `2` B, `3` C, the package order `Q1` uses), the base–emitter diode declared first and the collector `PwlCurve::Bjt { hfe, r_sat }` gated `AtLeast(0.65)` by the base; **IN-S63AS5UW** (Inolux IN-S63AS series V1.0: `V_F` 2.9 V typ / 2.7–3.1 V at 5 mA, 25 mA rating; key `IN-S63AS5UW`, the module's `MPN` field — `White` is a colour, not a key; pins `A`/`K`). `SS36` and `LTST-C190KGKT` as the solver half left them. **Lit** (`LibraryEntry::lit_amps`, `is_lit(current, i_on)`, `lit_threshold(key)`): the smallest current the datasheet distinguishes from a dark part — LTST-C190KGKT **10 µA**, its reverse-current maximum (BNS-OD-C131/A4 p. 3, Note 5), the one dark-current bound the sheet gives, so the 21 indicator LEDs at 5 mA read lit while the datasheet rates their brightness only at 20 mA (recorded as `IF_TEST`); IN-S63AS5UW **5 mA**, its test current, since its sheet tabulates no dark current (stated: the white entry calls the part lit only at rated brightness). No generic LED, diode or transistor entry.
   - **Three-region curves in the solver** (`cluster.rs`, completing the interrupted refactor): `PwlCurve::Regulator { i_reg, v_reg }` — cold start **is the ohmic segment** (a loop below the knee is consistent without a flip), on = `i_reg` beside the leakage; `PwlCurve::Bjt { hfe, r_sat }` with `Region::{Off, On, Active}` — the collector's region test is **current-based**: saturated while the load's current through `r_sat` is at most `hfe · I_B`, active — a current-controlled current source `hfe` times the base diode's own stamp, stamped as a transconductance on the base–emitter rows (the one unsymmetric stamp) — when it is not, and back to saturation when the source would pull the collector under the saturation line; `I_B` is the first diode declared from the control node to the emitter, so a collector with no base diode carries nothing; the region loop's `wanted` regions are computed once per solve and the first disagreement flips. `Region` replaces the solver-half's `bool`; a non-convergent solve reads every element `Off`. The active branch's reported current is the stamp's own, gain included (`ElementStamp::current`). Proofs: `cluster::tests::{a_polarity_fet_starts…, a_current_regulator_is_ohmic_below_its_knee_and_a_source_above_it (1 solve below the knee at V/280 Ω, 2 solves and 10 mA ± 23 nA of leakage above it), a_transistor_saturates_under_a_light_load_and_sags_under_a_heavy_one (1 kΩ: saturated in 3 solves, `V_C` = `I_C · 20 Ω`; 220 Ω: active in 4 solves, `I_C` = 100 `I_B` to 1e-4, the vertical base diode's cancellation)}`.
   - **The opto** (`models/src/opto.rs` `Opto`, one model for the VO2631 and the Lite-On 6N137): the LED is a `Branch` the component declares (the transducer primitive), read back through `on_branch` on its anode; the output sinks `0 V` through `V_OL(max)/I_OL` = 46.2 Ω while the LED carries ≥ `I_TH` (5 mA max, both sheets), `V_CC` reads up (VO2631 4.5 V, 80412 Recommended Operating Conditions; 6N137 4.5 V from the industry family sheet AV02-0940EN, since Lite-On's tabulates only its 5 V / 5.5 V test conditions — said so at the constant) and an enable, where the part has one, does not read low (`V_EL` 0.8 V; open = follows, the truth table's `NC` row); released otherwise, drive on change. The 6N137 facade carries **no pin 4**: the EdgeBoard's symbol has none. **The 6.92 Ω / 100 Ω tunings**: the VO2631's output impedance was `V_OL(typ)/I_OL` chosen so the *maximum* (46.2 Ω) would not fall within 10× of the datasheet's 330 Ω minimum pull-up and escalate an open-collector net; rule 2 ranks a 1 kΩ pull-up as a pull against any sink, so the tuning's reason is gone and the entry is the guaranteed bound, 46.2 Ω, the logic gates' convention. The ISO67xx's 100 Ω is `V_OL(max)/I_OL` from SLLSFJ6G §7.11 already (its "well clear of the ratio" remark was a consequence, not a choice) and stays.
   - **Terminals are boundaries for element membership** (`engine.rs` `build_topology`, `resolve_cluster`, `solve_cluster`, mirrored in the test-only reference): the decision the plan had not made, forced by the ROM boot. An element is a membership edge among its **non-terminal** nets (`element_home`: the cluster of its first non-terminal net in `a`, `b`, control order); a declared terminal it names — a rail, a harness supply, a `net_stuck` — stays outside and is handed to the solve as a **foreign** constant (`ClusterTopo::foreign`, appended to the solve's node list with its `ClusterTerminal`; a NaN rail stays out and the branch to it is dropped, a branch to no voltage); an element whose every net is a terminal is in no solve. A foreign terminal on a **conducting end** sources the cluster (a rail behind a diode is a rail: `on_request`, `PowerNetUnsourced`) but ranks nowhere (no resistive path reaches it); a terminal the **control alone** reads (`ClusterTopo::foreign_controls` — `U401`'s gate on the stuck ground) is a constant for the region test and sources nothing, which is what keeps the module's FET cluster, whose fingers are unstrapped on the ROM-boot bench, out of every solve. Why: with the previous rule `U401`'s gate on the stuck ground unioned `VIN_Edge`/`VIN_Edge_Protected` into the module's 8-root ground cluster, which then held an element and re-solved on every P59 edge of the boot — the 30 escalations the P2-package task measured for a second terminal in that cluster. With the rule the FET's cluster is `{VIN_Edge_Protected}` with the fingers and ground as constants, the twelve sinking LED chains under the bench rails are `{Y, anode}` (the §6 sizing) and ground's cluster holds no element — but not the nine chains sourced from `+3.3V`: a resistor edge still unions through a terminal, so those nine and the `D2` cathode meet on the rail as **one cluster of 20 roots and 10 elements** (19 unknowns, flip bound 20), re-solved on any of the nine inverters' toggles; the first draft of this record claimed `{Y, anode}` for every chain and a probe refuted it (the review record has the measurement, and `cluster_census::the_edge_board_under_the_bench_rails_has_a_largest_cluster_that_never_grows` holds the 20 as a never-rises figure with phase 4's 2 beside it). **`PowerNetUnsourced` gains a case**: a power pin whose net floats behind an element in a sourced cluster (a rail blocked by a reversed FET) is unsourced too — the finding `bench_straps_clear_the_domains_they_feed`'s reversed half asserts. Linear clusters are untouched (the four goldens pass without re-blessing); resistor edges still union through terminals until phase 4. Bare boards declare no terminal, so the census shows the collapse the rule prevents under rails: **EC32MB 79 / 10 / 11 stubs / 3 elements** (the FET's drain and source nets join the ground cluster through its gate; the two LED cathode nets join their anodes'), **Edge 167 / 29 / 4 stubs / 33 elements** (ground with the twelve sinking LED chains, the FET's nets and the charge-pump opto's anode net; the nine sourcing chains make `+3.3V` a 20-root cluster; the catch diodes stop at the bucks' NaN output nodes), **DS2 22 / 2 / 0 / 0**; `EDGE_MODELLED` names the five optos. Stubs left: EC32MB `U402`/`U403`, `U404`, `U501`–`U508`; Edge `U1`/`U2`, `IC3`/`IC4` — phase 4's.
   - **Proof landed**: `board/tests/board_elements.rs` (stepped, own binary, ten cases on the real EdgeBoard under `bench_rails`): `D3` lit at `(3.3 − 2.0) / (220 + R_OH)` within 1 % with its inverter's input low — within 15 % of the plan's `(3.3 − V_F)/220`, the gate's output resistance being the difference — the anode at 2.0 V, and dark with the input high (anode within a millivolt of ground, under a microamp); `U3` passing 12 V to `V_IN` within a millivolt with no current and no `PowerNetUnsourced`, and with the input 12 V below ground `V_IN` `Floating`, the FET under a microamp, `V_IN` reported unsourced; the upper end-switch loop (24 V on `J16.2`, a 0 V bench return on `J16.1`) at **10 mA ± 1 %** through `IC9` and `U6`'s LED alike, the LED at 1.38 V, `P19` `Driven(Low)`, and open: nothing through the regulator, `P19` at its pull-up; a VO2631 on a bench whose LED is 2 % under `I_TH` leaving its output `Pulled(High, 1000)` and 3 % over it `Driven(Low)`; `Q1` with `JP1` on its position, `P6` held high and a bench drive input (its own 5 V, a 1.38 V LED, a series resistor) on `SC_ENA`: base 0.65–0.75 V carrying `(V_OUTC − V_B)/43 kΩ` within 1 %, saturated under 1 kΩ (`I_C` = 3.62/1020 A, `SC_ENA` = `I_C · 20 Ω`) and active under 220 Ω (`I_C` = 100 `I_B` to 0.1 %, `SC_ENA` volts above ground where the load line puts it). `ec32mb_module::the_polarity_fet_passes_the_carrier_input_to_the_protected_rail`: `VIN_Edge_Protected` at 5.0 V from the fingers alone, nothing through the channel. `isolation_bridge` updated with the reason in-test: the enable path's base is a solved 0.65–0.75 V with its current, the unloaded collector sits at the emitter saturated and floats off (it read `Pulled(Low, 43 000)` and a model's switch before); the end-switch loop regulated at 10 mA where the stand-ins carried 27 mA from the 24 V rail and reported 10. `edgeboard::bench_straps_clear_the_domains_they_feed`'s dark half inverted: `V_IN` reads 12 V through the FET, and the reversed input is what leaves it unsourced. `machine_system`, `rs422_determinism`, `ec32mb_module::powered_module` and the P59 tests build with no FET line. The `Opto` unit tests (the truth table, the enable, drive on change), the library's (every entry's branches, the keys, the lit thresholds, the classification by MPN and by part name on both export forms). Census fixture updated as above; the four goldens pass without re-blessing.
   - **ROM boot** (`rom_boot_ec32mb`, `U401` and `D601`/`D602` live as elements): `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=0` (the wall figure this record carried, 0.247 s / 14.64 µs per edge, was the solver half's fast-path regression, corrected in the review pass and measured there) — the phase-0 counts to the edge; the FET's cluster has no source on this bench (the 5 V fingers are unstrapped, and its gate on the stuck ground is a control, not a source), the LED chains none (the buffer's outputs are released), and the ground cluster holds no element. Before the control rule the FET's cluster solved three times (the build, its fixed point, the start) on the strength of its gate's constant alone.
   - **What MaD will need** (unedited): `embsim_models::isolation::{nsi50010, vo2631, npn_switch, Nsi50010, Nsi50010Regulator, Vo2631, Vo2631Monitor, OptoChannel, NpnSwitch, NpnSwitchMonitor}` are gone — a consumer registers `embsim_models::opto::Opto::{vo2631, lite_on_6n137}` and `embsim_models::pwl_library::register` (which now carries the FETs, the transistor, the regulators and the white LED beside the diodes); `embsim_boards::ec32mb::POLARITY_FET_PINS` is gone (`Ec32mb::registry()` registers the library); `OptoChannel` lives in `embsim_models::opto`. `SIL/MaDSim` registers only the ADS122U04 and the machine parts and constructs none of these as far as this repository shows.
   *Review pass 2026-09-24.* Two reviewers read the two halves; every high and medium finding was verified against the code and fixed, the low ones where cheap, one reviewer's fix refused with the arithmetic. Every decision the plan had not made, written down:
   - **The fast path pays nothing for the current table** (`engine.rs`, `DESIGN.md` rule 8). `resolve_cluster` pushes an endpoint or element entry only when a solve produced one, or to clear a stored reading once when its cluster fell back to projection; `apply_currents` returns early on an empty pass and sets `currents_changed` only when a stored value differs (`same_current`); the engine publishes the shared table only then, by `copy_current_table_into` (no allocation after the first), and walks the instruments only after a publication, returning at once when none is subscribed; the cluster's terminals are collected as constants only in a cluster with elements. **A/B after** (`rom_boot_ec32mb`, five interleaved pairs, the same machine and minute as the bench below, six foreign processes at 100 % throughout — every absolute figure here is that load's, the ratios are the record): HEAD `deac7f5` wall 0.475 / 0.463 / 0.493 / 0.489 / 0.442 s (28.1 / 27.4 / 29.2 / 28.9 / 26.1 µs/edge) against this tree 0.466 / 0.511 / 0.497 / 0.476 / 0.435 s (27.6 / 30.2 / 29.4 / 28.2 / 25.7 µs/edge); medians 0.475 against 0.476 s; `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=0` in all ten. The +7 % the reviewers measured is gone.
   - **The linear solve has its old shape** (`cluster.rs`). One assembly closure (`assemble_linear`: edges, sources, injections, a constant read through `constant(root)` = `dirichlet.get(root)`, an empty table when no terminal was handed over) and two drivers: a cluster without an element assembles once, eliminates once by value and returns its states before any of the region machinery exists (no Dirichlet tables, no `voltages`, no stamps, no probe); the flip loop reuses the assembly and adds the elements' stamps. `solve_dense` takes the matrix by value again — the leakage probe no longer reads it. **`solve_bench` A/B** (release, `cargo run -p embsim-board --release --example solve_bench`, HEAD `deac7f5` in a detached worktree against this tree, 21 interleaved pairs in both orders, ns per solve, median of the runs' medians): m = 2: 1 254–1 272 against 1 329–1 360 (+4.5 to +8.5 %); m = 4: 2 000–2 028 against 2 118–2 162 (+5.8 to +7.4 %); m = 8: 4 316–4 432 against 4 372–4 478 (−0.4 to +3.3 %); m = 11: 6 548–6 650 against 6 668–6 803 (+1.7 to +2.8 %); m = 47: 74 772–75 648 against 75 156–78 177 (−0.7 to +4.4 %). Under this load one binary's own run-to-run medians spread ±10 % (HEAD m = 2: 1 102 to 1 413), so m ≥ 8 is indistinguishable from HEAD and the m = 2 / 4 reading — a consistent sign, ~50–120 ns — is below the noise floor but not proven gone; what the linear path still executes that HEAD did not is two empty `collect`s for the elements, a `chain` of the (empty) terminal seeds into the reach, one `get` on an empty table per node for the constant and one for the leakage mark, and a three-`Vec` `ClusterSolution` where HEAD returned one. **Open: a quiet-machine re-measure**; the figures beside phase 0's in §8 phase 0 are this run's.
   - **The leakage probe is structural** (`cluster.rs`; the plan's "a node only leakage reaches"). The `R_th` probe ran one full elimination per unknown after every converged element solve — O(m⁴), nineteen 19×19 eliminations per inverter toggle on the Edge `+3.3V` cluster — and its bound, `LEAKAGE_ONLY_OHMS` = 100 MΩ, made ten parallel leakages a source. Now: a reach from the sources and terminals over the resistor edges and the stamps that are more than the bare leakage (`g > 1/GMIN_OHMS`, a Norton current, a gain — a conducting region, a regulator's ohmic segment, a current source), O(m + edges), only after a converged element solve; a node the reach does not touch floats however many gigaohms meet there. `LEAKAGE_ONLY_OHMS` is gone. The unit cases (`a_node_reached_only_through_leakage_floats`, the reversed diode, the reversed FET on the Edge) read the same.
   - **A current instrument is a third read kind, not an analog sense** (`engine.rs` `ReadKind::{Digital, Instrument}`, `Resolver::add_current_instrument`, `ClusterTopo::current_instruments`; mirrored in `resolve_reference`, the build fixed point and the oracle's random boards). The solver half declared it `DeclareRead { kind: Analog }`, which put nine `FloatingSense { kind: Analog }` on the Edge board under the bench rails — every open opto loop, the bench's normal state (`Net-(IC6-K)` … `Net-(IC13-K)`, `Net-(U4-A)`) — and, by the operating-point precedence, silenced rule 2's fights in any linear cluster an instrument sat in. Now the instrument counts toward `on_request` exactly like an analog sense, is excluded from the floating-sense report, and takes no precedence: a cluster escalated by an instrument alone publishes the solve's voltages and reports its fights beside them, as an element cluster does. `board_elements::an_open_opto_loop_is_not_a_floating_input` holds it at build and live.
   - **A diode or LED symbol with no library entry is an unknown part** (`registry.rs` tier 1e; `NODES.md` §2, the Diode / LED row). The solver half left it to the parts task; the parts task registered every diode on both boards (every `D*` in `mad_edge.net` and `p2_ec32mb.net` carries a number the library keys) and did not decide; decided now, at no cost to the census (79 / 10 / 11 / 3, 167 / 29 / 4 / 33, 22 / 2 / 0 / 0 unchanged). `RegistryError::UnknownPart` gained `mpn: Option<String>` so the message names the number the export gave the part — the key an entry would take. `pwl_elements::a_diode_the_library_does_not_hold_is_an_unknown_part` asserts both symbols refused; the registry's `reference_fallback…` case reads `D601` with no library as `UnknownPart`.
   - **The record's sizing claim was wrong, and rule 4 has a gate.** Under the bench rails the twelve sinking LED chains are `{Y, anode}`; the nine sourced from `+3.3V` and the `D2` cathode are **one cluster of 20 roots and 10 elements** on `BENCH.3V3` (183 clusters, 48 escalated solves at build — the census case's own reading; the reviewers' probe had a live driver toggling `P26` → `U13` → `D7` two hundred times escalate 225 solves, 1.12 per toggle), because a resistor edge still unions through a terminal — phase 4's cut. `cluster_census::the_edge_board_under_the_bench_rails_has_a_largest_cluster_that_never_grows` holds the 20 as a never-rises figure with phase 4's 2 and rule 4's 8 named beside it; `DESIGN.md` rule 4's "Enforced by" says the bound is not met by phase 3 and becomes the gate in phase 4.
   - **Non-convergence floats past an unmodelled rail** (`engine.rs`, both arms; §7): an element cluster whose loop found no operating point assigns Floating to every non-terminal root before the NaN-rail "up through the path" presentation is consulted — that presentation is for a node nothing numeric reaches, not for a solve that failed. `pwl_elements::a_non_convergent_cluster_floats_even_where_an_unmodelled_rail_reaches` (the chase with a `PowerOut` stub rail one resistor away). And **`PowerNetUnsourced` is not raised per power pin of a non-convergent cluster** — `NonConvergent` names it once; a cluster that never solved (sourced by an unmodelled rail alone) reports as before, which `edgeboard::a_bare_board_reports_only_the_domains_that_arrive_over_a_cable` holds.
   - **`-0.0` is `0.0`** in `same_current` (engine and the build fixed point: `x + 0.0` folds the sign before `total_cmp`, so a `Drive::Current { amps: 0.0 }` slot's `-amps` and a released slot's `0.0` are one reading) and in the fought-terminal test (`cluster.rs`: `==` on two finite volts, so two terminals at `0.0` and `-0.0` are one voltage, not a Norton fight).
   - **The opto's enable band reads as disabled** (`models/src/opto.rs`, "Deliberate simplifications"): `LITE_ON_6N137_ENABLE_HIGH_MIN_VOLTS` = 2.0 V (`V_EH` minimum, Lite-On 6N137 Electrical–Optical Characteristics) is the bound the config carries (`OptoConfig::enable_high_min_volts`, replacing `enable_low_max_volts`, whose constant stays cited); an `Analog(v)` enable is enabled at `v ≥ V_EH` and released below it, the band included — the bound that guarantees the behaviour, the file's own convention; a level or an open enable as before.
   - **One reviewer's fix refused, with the arithmetic.** The On→Off test for a diode already on was to use the stamp's own current sign, `g·(V_a − V_b) − V_f·g ≥ 0`, in place of `V_a − V_b ≥ V_f`. With `g` = 1e6 exactly representable, `V_f·g` exact for the library's drops and `V_a − V_b` computed first, the product preserves the order of the difference against `V_f` for every non-zero margin and gives exactly zero for a zero one — the same comparison scaled, deciding identically at every ulp. The margin is real but not that: an on-segment at the floor into a far side only leakage loads has a drop of `V_f + I/g` with `I ≈ V/1 GΩ`, so the margin is `V·1e-15`, about four to five ulp of a node voltage at any supply scale; a fix would compute an on-diode's stay-on current from KCL at its node, or stamp `r_d` = 0 as a voltage-offset supernode merge — a larger change than a review pass makes. What landed: `cluster::tests::two_diodes_in_series_turn_on_one_after_the_other`, the case where the first diode is on into a far side only the second's leakage loads (exactly this state), converging in three solves with both on and one current; it is the guard on the regime.
   - **The rest**: `isolation_bridge` and `board_elements` assert the outcome their fixtures produce (`expect("… solves")` then `|amps| < 1e-9`, a reading of zero, never "no solve or zero"); the census module doc says which chains are two-net clusters and which are not; §7 records the vertical-segment cancellation ahead of the first element-bearing golden; the phase-4 entry names the `foreign` / `foreign_controls` fan-out. **The four goldens pass without re-blessing.**
   - **What MaD will need** (unedited): `RegistryError::UnknownPart` gained `mpn` (a full pattern needs it; no MaD-side pattern on it exists as far as this repository shows); `embsim_board::LEAKAGE_ONLY_OHMS` is gone; `OptoConfig::enable_low_max_volts` is `enable_high_min_volts`; a `LED` / `D_*` symbol on a MaD-side netlist with no library entry now refuses the build by name — register it, or `embsim_models::pwl_library::register` the boards' entries.
4. **Terminals, rails, supervisor, isolated domains — and `stub.rs` goes.** Declared terminals with fan-out (the property test extended first) — the terminal → dependent-cluster fan-out must include every cluster whose `ClusterTopo::foreign` or `foreign_controls` names the terminal (phase 3's element clusters stamp foreign terminal constants they are not members of, and today nothing changes a terminal's volts after build, so `dirty_scope`/`set_drive` never consult them), and the oracle's terminal-change extension must cover element clusters; `Rail`, `Supervisor`, UCC12040; `UnreferencedDomain`, `UndecoupledPowerPin` (needs the ref pin, so it lives here, not in phase 0), `RailDown`; the START gate; the I-V port. This phase classifies the last stub users (U402/U403/U404/U501–508, U1/U2/IC3/IC4), so `boards/src/stub.rs` and the test-side copies are deleted in it (`from_netlist_with_stubs` went in phase 1) and `stub_count` reads 0. Proof: EC32MB power tree reads Analog(3.3) ± 1 mV on every VIO and Analog(1.813) on Common_VDD from the J203 5 V/0 V fingers with no `net_stuck`, U402 and U403 different from one registry key; `p2_resn_is_sunk_until_common_vdd_crosses_1v6` asserts the t_SS instant; the ROM boot runs from the START gate with its `net_stuck` lines removed; `cluster_census` bounds max m ≤ 8; `bench_rails` shrinks to J2 + J21 and `the_whole_machine_builds_without_contention` still passes; IC14 GND2 → `UnreferencedDomain`; a mechanical pad on a net a pin drives → `Finding::MechanicalOnDrivenNet`, and the EC32 holes on Vss and the Edge holes on no net raise none; `sense_current` into a Low sink equals (3.3 − v)/R to 1 nA; build snapshot == pre-wake state on both boards; `no_board_contains_a_stub_part` == 0.
   *Done 2026-09-24* — three tasks, one phase: the **engine half** (terminals as one-node clusters with a fan-out table, `PowerOut` → terminal, the rule-4 bound), the **parts half** (`Rail`, `VoltageDetector`, the isolated domains, the four build lints, `stub.rs` and the test-side copies deleted, `bench_rails` shrunk) and the **START gate** (the P2 held until it can run, pads at their bank's supply, the ROM boot from the module's own power tree). The figures every later phase is measured against, every decision the plan had not made, the proof and the gates, in one place; the two halves' detailed records follow it, and the START-gate detail is the last set of bullets under this record.
   - **Figures.** Census, bare (`cluster_census.rs`, exact fixtures): **EC32MB 87 / 6 / 0 / 3** (from phase 3's 79 / 10 / 11 / 3), **Edge 176 / 29 / 0 / 33** (from 167 / 29 / 4 / 33), **DS2 22 / 2 / 0 / 0**; `stub_count` 0 on every board (`no_board_contains_a_stub_part`, a never-rises gate). Rule 4's bound under the reference harnesses (`every_board_under_its_reference_harness_is_within_rule_4s_bound`): EC32MB from its `J203` fingers **4** (92 clusters, 1 escalation at build — the FET), DS2 under `force_domain_rails` **2**, the machine with the module in its socket under `bench_rails` + `force_domain_ground` **4** (255 clusters, 65 escalations at build); the Edge board alone under the bench rails 193 clusters, largest **9** (`VIO_16_23`, a socket finger the module's LDO sources), a never-rises fixture above the bound, and with that finger held at the LDO's 3.3 V — the Edge board's reference harness on the bench alone, the review pass — 201 clusters, largest **3**. **ROM boot** (`rom_boot_ec32mb`, the module powered from `J203`'s two 5 V and three 0 V fingers, **nothing stuck**): `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953` — phase 0's counts to the edge — `start_ns=2500000` (the START instant: `U402`'s `t_SS`, where `Common_VDD` steps to 1.8133 V and the detector releases `P2_RESN` to `Pulled(High, 10 500)` in the same instant, no crossing to delay), the first flash-clock edge at 2 867 900 ns (367.9 µs of ROM after START, the RNG seeding and the strap reads), `crystal_hz()` **`Some(20_000_000)`** on the node and the package (the TCXO up from the core rail), `escalated_solves=5` exactly — the polarity FET's element cluster three times at t = 0 (the start pass, then once per full pass that applies a batch of the QEMU core's 64 pad-read declarations, every element solve cold) and the two feedback dividers once each at 2.5 ms — **none per edge**; **60 000 / 60 000 states identical to p2core** (the README recipe, run 2026-09-24 against the START-gated boot: `compared 60000 | dropped 0 | identical`); wall 0.30–0.62 s across runs under a foreign load average of 4–14 (the counts are the record). Goldens: `serial_levels`, `wake_ladder`, `nominal_analog_cluster` byte-identical throughout the phase; `net_stuck_shared_node` re-blessed once, by the engine half, 65 → 17 records with the diff reviewed below, and unchanged since.
   - **Decisions the plan had not made**, the smallest choice each time (the halves' records carry the measurements): (1) `PowerOut` idle `KindDefault` stayed unmodelled until the last facade went, and `IdleDrive::{Released, Thevenin}` are honoured on `PowerOut`; (2) a fought terminal is decided once, at its own one-node cluster, and held for every dependent; (3) a terminal-to-terminal edge belongs to no cluster, a released terminal sources nothing; (4) the rule-4 bound is asserted on every board under its reference harness — its declared supplies; the Edge board's on the bench alone is the bench rails plus its one module-sourced socket finger at the module LDO's 3.3 V (the review pass; the phase had first asserted the Edge board only with the module in its socket, which redefined the bound rather than met it); (5) `ref_root` stayed out of the engine — the reference is `Component::references()` beside `branches()` until §12 item 5 moved it onto `PinDecl`; (6) the census build-solve bound 64 → 80; (7) every rail input is a `PowerIn` sense, not `Analog` (one escalation each per pass against the boot's budget, measured); (8) the inductor is an identity union, not a 0 Ω edge (the `+3.3V` collapse, measured); (9) an isolated ground is declared and never driven — a domain nothing ties has no reference and its rail stays down, named, never "0 V of its own frame"; (10) the four lints are derived by the build from settled states and declarations, no model-side report path; (11) no soft-start figure invented for the NCP114's 3.3 V option or the XL1509 (a step at the enable instant, the omission stated), the STM1061 released below the 0.7 V it is guaranteed from, its `t_PD`/`t_PR` scheduled instants; (12) the STM1061 cites the reachable Rev 2.0 copy, ST's Rev 4 being unreachable while it was written; (13) the assembled-machine proof is its own binary (the add-on's ADC thread idle-jumps every later stepped case's clock); (14) **the START gate is the package's** — on `P2Core::start` and on the wakes a core routes through `P2Pads` (`on_wake_ns`, now `Sync`; `schedule_at_ns` held until started and landed at `max(at, START)`) — core-agnostic, so the QEMU core, MaD's ISS and the native image are gated alike; `VDD` is read as the voltage its `PowerIn` sense delivers (`Analog(v)` at path 0 from a terminal, decision 7's rule; a `PinKind::Analog` `VDD` was not declared), the window `[1.7, 1.9]` V inclusive (P2X8C4M64P Datasheet, DC Characteristics, p. 47, the `Vdd` row), a `VDD` naming a level and no voltage (`Driven`, `Pulled`) outside it, `RESN` by `level_of`; `P2ResetState` gained `vdd_volts` (and lost `Eq`), `out_of_reset()` is the gate's condition; (15) **the datasheet's 3 ms restart delay is not modelled** ("Propeller restarts 3 ms after RESn transitions from low to high", Pin Descriptions, p. 6): a scheduled instant needs a wake of the package's own, and the native core registers its wake handler on the net I/O directly — last registration wins — so a package wake would hold the QEMU core and not the native one; the gate opens the instant its conditions hold, the omission is in `boards/src/p2.rs`'s "does not do yet" list, and the datasheet's "within 5 ms loads its Bootloader" is a bound, not an instant; §12 item 5's P2 task closed it (a wake of the package's own, every core's time through the package's `WakeGate`); (16) **the report is handle-readable, not an engine `Finding`** (decision 10 kept: no model-side report path): `P2PackageHandle::start_state()` reads `Held { reset }` with the inputs as last read or `Started { at_ns }`, `unpowered_banks_driven()` the banks a core drove without a supply — `tracing::info` at hold and at START, `tracing::warn` once per bank — the shape the phase-2 crystal stall reports in; the build already names an unsourced `VIO_a_b` (`PowerNetUnsourced`); (17) `P2Core::start` runs at the START instant on whichever thread opened the gate (the engine thread from a `RESN`/`VDD` sense, the starting thread when the inputs already allowed it at `Component::start`), before any held wake is forwarded, so the QEMU core anchors clock segment 0 at `virtual_ns()` inside `start` — on a bench with its supplies up from the build that is 0, every bench instant unchanged; the core lives in `Arc<Mutex<C>>` for that, and `P2Package::core()` (no user) is gone; the package's own wake forwarder is registered **before** the core attaches, so a native core's direct registration replaces it and its wakes run as before; (18) **pads at their bank's supply**: `BankSupplies` (sixteen atomics of `f64` bits, NaN for no voltage; one load per pad decode), `P2Pads::bank_supplies()`, `BankSupplies::pad_drive(pin, cfg, dir, out)` beside the free `pad_drive(cfg, dir, out, high_volts)`; the bank is `pin / 4` by the `VIO_a_b` names (Pin Descriptions, p. 6, "in groups of 4"); a bank whose pin reads `Floating`, `Contention` or a digital projection names no voltage and drives nothing, high or low; the `Vxxyy` window (3.15–3.45 V, the same table) is **not** applied to the drive — a pad drives at what its pin reads (the plan's 1.8 V bench case); the native core (`McuComponent`) still drives at `LOGIC_HIGH_VOLTS` through the HAL bridges, which publish on the pads' handles directly — closed by §12 item 5's P2 task (`McuComponent::host_pads`); (19) the two QEMU benches (`pad_modes`, `crystal_pll`) supply `VDD` 1.8 V, `RESN` 3.3 V and `VIO_0_3` 3.3 V — rule 6, a bench P2 has its supplies named — and every figure they assert is unchanged; (20) the ROM boot's escalation budget is **5, exactly and accounted**, not 0: a boot whose module makes its own rails pays the power tree's solves once, before its first edge, and none per edge (the phase-0 gate's meaning); the three FET solves at t = 0 are the phase-2 review's read-declaration batching (one full pass per batch, each cold-starting the element) — a start-time cost, the fast path untouched, and coalescing the batches is a follow-up nobody needs yet; (21) `P2_FAST_OHMS` keeps its 25 Ω placeholder and its TODO now names the datasheet figures it will come from (`Voh` −170 / −580 mV at 10 / 30 mA, `Vol` 160 / 510 mV: 17–19 Ω, DC Characteristics, p. 47) — every projection on the boards ranks a fast pad against 10 kΩ and more, so the figure decided nothing; §12 item 5's P2 task fitted it, 17.99 Ω (the rows are on p. 48, the table's continuation).
   - **Deferred to phase 4 by phases 2–3, and where each stands.** Closed: the START gate (the phase-2 P2 record's "lands with the rails"); `P2ResetState`'s window ("the datasheet's `VDD` range is the START gate's to cite"); a pad's high at its bank's `VIO` ("until the bank rails are modelled"); the ROM boot's crystal `None` → `Some(20 MHz)` and its cause assertion (flipped by the parts half, the cause itself gone with the `net_stuck` lines); the two `net_stuck` lines; the eleven facades and `stub.rs`; the terminal fan-out and the oracle's terminal ops; `UnreferencedDomain`, `UndecoupledPowerPin`, `RailDown`, `MechanicalOnDrivenNet`; the rule-4 bound as a gate; `bench_rails` = J2 + J21. Open, with an owner: `Finding::BrownoutWithoutReset` (a `VDD` or `RESN` that drops after START is delivered as a reset state and changes nothing — no core has a reset entry; the `p2-qemu` vtable's reset entry); the 3 ms restart delay (decision 15); the native core's pads through `BankSupplies` (decision 18); `P2_FAST_OHMS` (decision 21) — all four taken up by §12 item 5's P2 task, whose record says how each stands; the current-source pad modes (no caller — `Drive::Current` is their encoding when one exists); an `UndecoupledPowerPin` bench fixture (the lint has declarations and no board case, a follow-up); the quiet-machine re-measure of `solve_bench` and the boot's wall time (environmental, open since phase 3; counts are the record).
   - **Proof landed** (this task's; the halves' below): `board/tests/p2_package.rs`, 17 cases stepped in its own binary — the START gate (`the_core_is_started_when_vdd_enters_its_window_and_held_outside_it`: a recording core that asks for a wake at 1 ns; `RESN` from the bench and a bench rail on `VDD` released at the build and rising at 2 ms to 1.8 / 1.7 / 1.9 V — started at exactly 2 000 000 ns with the first wake at that instant — or to 1.2 / 2.5 V — never started, no wake, `Held` with the rail's voltage readable; `the_core_is_held_while_resn_is_low_with_vdd_in_its_window`; the reset-state cases extended with a `VDD` under and over the window and the gate's verdict on every case), pads at their bank's supply (`a_pad_drives_high_at_its_banks_supply_and_nothing_in_an_unpowered_bank`: a core driving `P4` fast with `VIO_4_7` at 1.8 V from the bench — an analog probe reads 1.8 V — and `P8` with `VIO_8_11` unconnected — floating, `unpowered_banks_driven() == [2]`, `VIO_8_11`); `boards/src/p2.rs` unit tests (the window at its bounds and just outside, the bank grouping per pin, the table's drive and its once-per-bank report); `p2-qemu` unit tests (a pad at a 1.8 V bank, a pad released when every supply goes); `rom_boot_ec32mb` as above (`started_at_ns() == Some(2_500_000)`, `out_of_reset()`, no edge before START, the core rail at 1.8133 V ± 1 mV, 20 MHz on both handles, no bank driven unpowered, five solves); `crystal_pll` asserts `started_at_ns() == Some(0)`.
   - **Gates** (this task's run, verbatim in its report): `cargo fmt`, `clippy -D warnings`, `cargo doc -D warnings` clean; `embsim-board`/`boards`/`models`/`cffi` all binaries green; `determinism` 24 passed, no re-bless; `embsim-p2-qemu` 13 unit + `crystal_pll` + `pad_modes` + `rom_boot_ec32mb` green with the baseline line above; `vibes collect --write && vibes lint` 481 behaviours, numbers to BH-571, 0 errors (the 51 warnings are the pre-existing set). One thing the ledger's collection taught: the collector runs `cargo test -p embsim-board` fail-fast and keeps whatever declared before the first failing binary, silently — one `power_tree_machine` failure under a load spike (five clean reruns after it) dropped every binary after it from the working-tree ledger, and the next collect re-numbered the 58 dropped rows as new; the numbers were restored from `HEAD` (34 keys) and, for `terminals.rs`'s keys, from the collector's own rule (new keys numbered in sorted-key order from `behaviours.next`, 492–515 after the engine half's three census keys), verified by a further collect that kept every row and number. A partial run ought to be a collector error — a Vibes note, upstream.
   - **What MaD will need** (unedited, beside the halves' lists): `P2Core::start` runs at the START instant on the engine thread when a sense opens the gate — a MaD core's `start` must not assume the `System::start` thread; `P2Pads::on_wake_ns` requires `Sync`; `P2ResetState` has a third field, `vdd_volts`, and no `Eq`; `P2Package::core()` is gone; `SIL/p2iss`'s `P2Core` (the phase-2 need, still open there) takes the bank table — `pads.bank_supplies().pad_drive(pin, cfg, dir, out)` where `pad_drive(cfg, dir, out, LOGIC_HIGH_VOLTS)` would have gone — and a MaD bench with a P2 package on it supplies `VDD`, `RESN` and the banks it drives, as the two QEMU benches now do; a MaD system with the module from `J203` sees its core start at 2.5 ms.
   *Review pass 2026-09-24.* Two reviewers read the three tasks; every high and medium finding was verified against the tree before it was acted on — reproduced where it was a race — and the low ones taken where cheap. What each found, and what changed:
   - **The START gate could open before the bank table was told** (high, `boards/src/p2.rs`). Reproduced first: 2 of 15 runs of the `p2_package` binary failed `a_pad_drives_high_at_its_banks_supply…` with `P2.P4 … got Floating`. `P2Package::attach` registered the `RESN`/`VDD` senses before the sixteen `VIO_a_b` senses; the engine delivers a sense once at registration, in registration order, and `System::start` runs `Component::start` on the caller's thread meanwhile — so the gate could open at the `VDD` delivery, or from `start`, with the bank registrations still queued, and `BankSupplies::pad_drive` read NaN and released the pad. Fixed both ways the reviewer named: the bank senses are registered **before** the two the gate reads, and `BankTable` carries a 16-bit `delivered` mask — `BankSupplies::all_delivered`, a third condition of `try_begin`, which every bank delivery now also calls so the condition can never stall the gate. Measured, 30 runs each: reorder + mask **0/30**; the mask with the original order **5/30** — `the_reset_state…::case_1` read `Held` with an out-of-reset reason between the `VDD` delivery and the sixteenth bank's, the gate opening a delivery late — so the order decides *when* the gate opens (at the reset delivery) and the mask *what it opens onto* (a told table). Decision (22): the gate's third condition is "every bank told", not the plan's "the boot banks' VIO up": a bench P2 (`pad_modes`, `crystal_pll`) supplies one bank and would never start under the plan's wording, and "told" is what the failure needed; the ROM boot's START instant (2.5 ms, opened by `VDD`) is unchanged. Unit case `the_bank_table_is_delivered_only_once_every_bank_has_been_told`; the integration proof is the 0/30.
   - **Rule 4's bound was redefined rather than met** (medium, `DESIGN.md`, `cluster_census.rs`). The Edge board alone under `bench_rails` holds `VIO_16_23` + `P16`–`P23` as a 9-root cluster and the bound had been asserted only with the module in the socket. Fixed as the reviewer proposed: `cluster_census::edge_reference_rails` is `bench_rails` plus the one socket finger a module sources — `J3.58` (`mad_edge.net` net 190, `V16`) — held at the module LDO `U503`'s 3.3 V, the voltage its value names (`LDO 300mA, 3.3V`; a declared harness terminal standing in for the part, rule 6). `every_board_under_its_reference_harness_is_within_rule_4s_bound` asserts it as a fourth system: **201 clusters, largest 3** (`Net-(IC14-OUTC)`, `Net-(JP1-B)`, `Net-(Q1-B)`), 63 escalations at build. `DESIGN.md` rule 4's "Enforced by" reads **m ≤ 8 on every board** again, each under its declared supplies, the four figures 4 / 2 / 3 / 4, and the 9 named as above the bound and held as a never-rises fixture that says so. Decision (4) is amended above. `bench_rails` itself is unchanged: the Edge-alone tests that read `P16`–`P23` floating with their bank rail named keep their claim, and the census says that state is above the bound and why.
   - **`p2_resn_is_sunk_until_common_vdd_crosses_1v6` raced** (high, `power_tree.rs`). The 1.8 V iteration's wait was satisfied by the build state — the stuck bank rail through the pull-up, the detector's output idle released — before the detector's supply senses were delivered on the engine thread, so `asserted()` read `None` (the reviewer's 7/10). Fixed by waiting on the pair that is asserted, the verdict and the node. The 30-run loop then found a third race, `sense_current_into_a_low_sink…` (1/30: node `Analog(0)`, current 0 A — the state and current tables are published separately); it waits on the pair too, the rail and the findings in its message, and reads 0/60 alone and 0/20 whole-binary since. The full gate then failed once more, in `power_tree_machine` (`VIO_16_23 reads 0`) — the failure the START-gate task had put down to a load spike, and it is not one: on the module every LDO's `IN` and `EN` sit on `Common_LDOin` (`p2_ec32mb.net`), the two senses are delivered `IN` first, and between the two deliveries — one pass, one instant — the NCP114 is input-up with its enable still floating-to-off and publishes its 100 Ω active discharge, `Analog(0)` on the bank rail, before the `EN` delivery lifts it to 3.3 V; a superseding publish at one instant (rule 3), invisible on the wire, visible to a wall-clock poller waiting on `Analog(_)`; and the eight LDOs publish one after another as their senses are delivered, so a poller that waited on one bank rail could read the next still `Floating` (6/30 once the first wait was tightened). The rail waits in `power_tree` and `power_tree_machine` now wait on every module rail at its setpoint (`module_rails_up`) before reading any, and say why; 0/40 and 0/20 after.
   - **Two module claims were documented as moved and were not** (medium). Landed in `power_tree.rs`: `the_reset_node_floats_without_its_pull_up_once_the_rails_are_up` — the module from its fingers with `R100.1` detached, stepped past `t_SS`: `VIO_56_63` at 3.3 V, the detector released with its verdict at 2 500 000 ns, `P2_RESN` `Floating` and `FloatingSense` on it, the carrier's "module never boots" — and, in `the_ec32mb_power_tree_reads_its_rails…`, `P2_IO62_TXD`/`P2_IO63_RXD` at `Pulled(High, 100 000)` once the bank rail is up. The two `ec32mb_module.rs` doc comments now point at claims that exist.
   - **`UndecoupledPowerPin` had no proving test** (medium). `power_tree::a_supply_pin_with_no_capacitor_to_its_reference_is_reported`: a bench part declaring `VCC` referenced to `GND` — no capacitor → reported naming part, pin and reference; `C1` across the two nets → not; `C1` from `VCC` to a third net → reported; and the EC32MB from its fingers and the Edge board under the bench rails raise **none** (measured: no false positive on the buck, detector, isolator or ISO67xx inputs today). Ledger key `lint.undecoupled-power-pin`.
   - **The ROM boot's escalation budget, 0 → 5** (medium/low, a lead decision the reviewers asked for). Kept at 5 as recorded — between accepting and coalescing the read batches the record chooses nothing — with a measurement added that bears on it: the count read **6** on the one run that overlapped a full `cargo test` of four crates (wall 1.26 s) and **5** on every other — four quiet runs in each registration order, six under eight busy loops, and the gate runs. The three FET solves at t = 0 are one per full pass that applies a batch of read declarations, and how the QEMU core's 128 attach-time commands fall into the engine's 64-command drain batches depends on how the main thread's sends interleave with the engine's drains — fixed in practice on a quiet machine, once in twelve runs here not. Coalescing (one full pass for every read declared before the first wake, or carrying element solutions across a rebuild whose only change is added senses) would make the count a function of the inputs alone — 3 — and is the follow-up the lead decides; until then the gate is exact and its one known sensitivity is written here. `NODES.md` §6 now names the two divider solves at the rail's rise where it said "rails add no solves".
   - **Low findings taken.** `NODES.md` §2's Rail and Supervisor rows say `PowerIn`/`DigitalIn` as the models declare. A `PowerOut` pin's `IdleDrive::Thevenin` keeps its impedance on the slot: `Resolver::add_terminal_endpoint` takes the idle as the `Option<Drive>` the slot holds (`TerminalDrive::idle_slot_drive` for the two 0 Ω encodings), asserted by `engine::tests::a_terminal_slots_idle_drive_keeps_its_declared_impedance`. The Rail's input and analog-enable latches decide nothing against a ground no source reaches — `input_up = false`, `enabled = None` (evaluates disabled), a level on the enable read as before — one unit case, the simplification listed in the module doc; the detector's `sink_drive` `unwrap_or(0.0)` is unreachable (a verdict needs `VSS` read) and stays. A bank supply changing after START is in the package's "does not do yet" list. The two START-gate negatives are proved at a bench wake the engine fired (`Tick` at the rail's instant + 1 ms, or at 1 ms) instead of a 50 ms sleep. `ds2_regressions::the_referenced_add_on…` declares `lint.referenced-add-on-reports-nothing`.
   - **Gates** (this pass's run, verbatim in its report): `cargo fmt`, `clippy -D warnings`, `cargo doc -D warnings` clean; `embsim-board`/`boards`/`models`/`cffi` 50 binaries, 1108 passed, 0 failed, 5 ignored; `determinism` 24 passed, no re-bless (the four goldens as the phase left them); `embsim-p2-qemu` 13 unit + `crystal_pll` + `pad_modes` + `rom_boot_ec32mb` green, `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=5 start_ns=Some(2500000)`; `vibes collect --write && vibes lint` 490 behaviours, numbers to BH-581, 0 errors, 51 warnings (the pre-existing set; this pass's nine new keys are BH-573–581 — the working tree's `behaviours.next` read 572 with no row at 572, so the collector's rule numbered from 573 — no row dropped, none renumbered). Loops, the races' proof: `p2_package` 0/30 (2/15 before), `power_tree` 0/20 after 0/60 on the current case alone, `power_tree_machine` 0/40 (6/30 with one rail waited on). Wall figures are the machine's that day: the ROM boot 0.41–0.47 s quiet, 0.55–0.65 s under eight busy loops, 1.26 s under a concurrent full test build; counts are the record.
   - **What MaD will need** (unedited): a MaD core inside `P2Package` sees `P2Core::start` only after all sixteen `VIO_a_b` senses have delivered — once, at registration, so nothing to do MaD-side; `Resolver::add_terminal_endpoint` is `pub(crate)` and its signature change reaches nothing outside this repository.
   *Engine half done 2026-09-24* (terminals as one-node clusters with a fan-out table, `PowerOut` → terminal, and the rule-4 census bound; the `Rail`, `Supervisor` and UCC12040 models, `UnreferencedDomain`/`UndecoupledPowerPin`/`RailDown`, the START gate, `MechanicalOnDrivenNet`, the boot's `net_stuck` lines and `stub.rs`'s deletion were the next tasks'). What landed, and every decision the plan had not made, written down as the smallest choice that let the work continue:
   - **The property test first, and what it found** (`engine::tests::incremental_oracle`, 2000 cases): the op sequence gained `Op::Terminal` — a declared terminal source re-set to a voltage, released or unmodelled (`Resolver::set_terminal`) — and `Op::Rail` — a `PowerOut` slot published to or released (`add_terminal_endpoint` + `set_drive`) — over random boards that now also place `PowerOut` pins, beside the slot drives. On the phase-3 tree it failed **twice, and earlier than the entry predicted**: (1) `ClusterTopo` cached `(root, volts)` for `power`/`stuck`/`foreign`/`foreign_controls` at build, so a changed terminal was invisible even to its own cluster — the shrunk case a lone `net_stuck` at 0 V re-set to 3.3 V, incremental `Analog(0.0)` against the reference's `Analog(3.3)`; (2) with the cache reading live volts, the predicted one — a diode from a stuck 3.3 V anode to a pad-driven cathode reads 2.55 V after the terminal drops to 0 V while the reference reads 0.0, because the element cluster stamping the terminal as a foreign constant was never dirtied (`dirty_scope`/`set_drive` consulted no fan-out). It passes on this tree.
   - **Terminals as one-node clusters, everywhere** (`engine.rs`; `NODES.md` "Three rules the taxonomy rests on", 1): `build_topology` unions no conduction edge through a terminal root — an edge belongs to the cluster of its non-terminal end, one between two terminals to none — so every terminal root is a cluster of its own (`ClusterTopo::terminal`), and every cluster carries `boundary` (the terminals its edges end on and its elements' conducting ends name, ascending) and `boundary_controls`, with `dist` widened to `roots × (roots + boundary)`. `Topology::terminals` records, per terminal in root order, its sources in canonical order and its **fan-out** (`dependents`: every cluster whose `boundary` or `boundary_controls` names it); `set_drive` on a `PowerOut` slot and `set_terminal` dirty the terminal's own cluster and its fan-out (`mark_terminal_dirty`), so `resolve_dirty == full pass` survives. A pass decides the dirty terminals first — `resolve_terminal` → `decide_terminal`, one function shared with the reference — into `TerminalState` per terminal, then resolves the clusters, each reading its own terminal and its boundary as the one state decided: a Dirichlet constant of its solve, **linear clusters included** (the phase-3 `ClusterTerminal` path, no longer confined to element clusters), and an ideal 0 Ω source of its ranking through the path to it (`project_root` still sees a terminal at path 0 as `Analog(v)` on the root). `ensure_topology` clears the terminal states and `terminals_resolved`, so a rebuilt topology's next pass is full (`dirty_scope` says every net). The test-only reference resolver mirrors all of it with its own DSU, boundary lists and per-root `min_path_ohms`, sharing only `project_root` and `decide_terminal`. Rule 8: a linear cluster whose terminals did not change is not in any fan-out walk and pays nothing new; a cluster with one reaching source still projects without a solve (`terminals::the_p59_pull_down_projects…`).
   - **`TerminalDrive::{Released, Unmodelled, Volts}`** — the three things a declared source can hold, and `decide_terminal`'s reconciliation: no voltage → unmodelled if any source is (the facade's NaN, presented as up through the path to it, as before), else **released** (a rail that is down: its node floats, nothing reaches its dependents through it, its `PowerIn` loads are `PowerNetUnsourced`, and a bench strap on the same net sources it without contention); agreeing voltages (`==`, so `0.0` and `-0.0` are one) → that voltage; disagreeing → **fought**: solved once as a one-node cluster of the ideal sources (the same solver, counted as the escalation it is), held for every dependent at the fight's operating point, and reported **once**, at the terminal's own cluster — `Contention` + `AmbiguousLevel(v)` inside the dead band, `Analog(v)` outside, naming the strong slots that fought it too if any (a terminal has no pin) — unless an analog sense reads the node, whose phase-1 precedence is kept. Two dependents, one finding (`terminals::two_sources_that_disagree_on_a_terminal_fight_once`: the resistor-fed node reads the fight's 1.65 V, the diode-fed node one knee below it, two solves at build — the fight's and the diode cluster's). The solver's own fought-pair path (`cluster.rs`) stays for a caller that hands a pair directly; the resolver never does now.
   - **`PowerOut` → terminal** (`system.rs` `add_pin_descriptor`): a `PowerOut` pin gets a drive slot flagged `terminal` (`Resolver::add_terminal_endpoint`), so `PinHandle::drive`/`release` set what the rail holds — a `Thevenin`'s open-circuit voltage (its impedance recorded on the slot for the I-V port, not solved, `NODES.md` §2), a `Current` or a release lets it go — and the slot is never a source of the cluster it sits in and carries no current (`PinHandle::carries_current` is false on a terminal, no `CurrentPort` is registered for it, `on_branch` is refused at attach: `terminals::a_current_instrument_is_refused_on_a_power_out_pin`). Membership is fixed at build: the net is a terminal whatever the pin holds. **The idle** — decided here: `IdleDrive` is honoured on `PowerOut` (`validate_idle_drives` refuses it on `PowerIn`/`Passive` only, `BoardError::IdleOnSlotlessPin` keeps its name): `Released` → the rail is down from build, `Thevenin(v)` → up at `v` from build, `KindDefault` → **unmodelled**, today's NaN presentation. Why the default is not released: the eleven facades left (U402/U403, U404, U501–U508 on the module; U1/U2, IC3/IC4 on the Edge board) declare no voltage and every supply gate reads the projection (`isolation::supply_up`, the LDO enables, the P2's `VIO` senses), so a released default would read every rail as down one task early; the Rail model retires the default with the last facade. The slot's idle drive is the NaN-volt Thevenin `PowerOut` has always meant, so a model that releases at attach is a change the build's fixed point sees. **`ref_root` is not in the engine**: `PinDecl` carries no `reference` yet (§11), a rail model reads `V(ref)` on its own ground pin through the sense it already has and publishes the absolute voltage, and `UnreferencedDomain` needs the model-side report path — left to the Rail task with this reasoning; the engine's terminal holds an absolute voltage.
   - **Census** (`cluster_census.rs`, the fixture updated exactly; measured against the phase-3 tree cluster by cluster): bare **EC32MB 87 / 8 / 11 / 3** (from 79 / 10: the bucks' `SW` nodes are `PowerOut` terminals of their own, so the output inductors end on them and the ground cluster is the eight roots `NODES.md` §6 sized — `GND`, `Common_VDD`, `Common_LDOin`, the two `FB` nodes, the P59 pull-down net, `VIN_Edge`/`VIN_Edge_Protected`; the LDO outputs left the clusters their pull-ups formed — `VIO_56_63`'s 7-root cluster with `S301-3_OFF`, `SPI_CS`, the debug-serial and the RESN pull-ups is `{P2_RESN, P2_RESN_PROTECTED}` and five singletons, `{P2_IO57, VIO_40_47}` two), **Edge 169 / 29 / 4 / 33** (from 167: `Net-(D1-K)` and `Net-(D2-K)`, U1/U2's outputs, are terminals, so `L1`/`L2` end on them and `+5V`/`+3.3V` lost them — the `+3.3V` cluster reads 19; ground's 29 is unchanged, a bare board declaring no ground), **DS2 22 / 2 / 0 / 0**. Under the bench rails the Edge board's nine `+3.3V` chains are `{anode, cathode}` — the plan's 2, asserted per chain (`the_edge_board_under_the_bench_rails_has_two_root_led_chains_and_a_largest_cluster_that_never_grows`), `BENCH.3V3` a cluster of its own, the build's escalations 72 (from 48: nine two-root solves and their re-solves where one 19-unknown solve was; bound 80), and the **largest cluster is 9**, not 2: `VIO_16_23`, the P2 bank rail the module's LDO sources — a socket finger on the board alone — with `P16`–`P23` pulled up to it through `R1`–`R8`, a never-rises fixture with its reason. **The rule-4 bound is asserted under each board's reference harness** (`every_board_under_its_reference_harness_is_within_rule_4s_bound`): the EC32MB from its `J203` 5 V/GND fingers **4** (`DIPSW_LEDPWR` with `R601` and U601's outputs; 92 clusters, 1 escalation at build — the FET), the DS2 under `force_domain_rails` **2**, and the Edge board **with the module in its socket** under `bench_rails` **4** (257 clusters, 75 escalations at build). The decision: the bench supplies no module rail — it is the LDO's — so the Edge board alone cannot meet 8 on that one finger, and the bound is held where the rail has its declared source; bare boards, which declare no ground (§2 "Ground": the bench return is a harness terminal), stay fixtures. `DESIGN.md` rule 4's "Enforced by" now says exactly that; the rule itself is unchanged.
   - **Goldens**: `serial_levels` and `wake_ladder` byte-identical; `nominal_analog_cluster` byte-identical (no terminal in it); **`net_stuck_shared_node` re-blessed, 65 → 17 records** — every changed line: records 0–5 are unchanged (the build's three `net_resolved … analog:0uv`, `reroute`, the registration `sense`, `drive_applied seq=0`); after each of the twelve `drive_applied` records the four that followed it — `net_resolved net=0/1/2 state=analog:0uv` and `sense net=2 state=analog:0uv` — are gone, 48 records in all, because the stuck node is now held at exactly 0 V by Dirichlet elimination where the Norton floor left it a few nanovolts off and a fresh bit pattern under each drive; `same_state` sees no change, so nothing is republished or delivered. The twelve `drive_applied` records remain, in order. Physics and published levels unchanged: every quantized state was and is 0 µV, the node is stuck, and the analog probe reads what it read.
   - **ROM boot** (`rom_boot_ec32mb`, with today's two `net_stuck` lines): `edges=16901 yields=16953 publishes=16953 escalated_solves=0` — the counts to the edge; `crystal_hz()` still `None` and `Common_VDD` still `Pulled(Low, 23 800)` (the cause assertion, for the Rail task to flip). The boot alone cannot show the 30-escalation configuration (it holds no core rail), so **`terminals::the_p59_pull_down_projects_beside_a_held_core_rail_on_the_module`** does, stepped: the module with a bench 0 V on `J203.43`, `Common_VDD` stuck at 1.8 V, pole 4 closed, and a bench pad on `J203.53` toggling P59 two hundred times — `Net-(U402-FB)` reads the divider's 0.794 V from the build on, the escalation count reads 2 before the edges (the build's and the start's full pass, one divider solve each) and 2 after, P59 `Driven` to each level and `Pulled(Low, 10 500)` released: the divider is a cluster of its own between two terminals and P59's is another. Wall: `wall=0.498s (29.48 us/edge)` on a machine whose load average sat at 10–14 throughout (foreign processes; the phase-3 review measured 0.475 s for the same counts under a comparable load and 0.21 s quiet), so the figure is the load's and the counts are the record. `solve_bench` (release, same load): 1 424 / 2 248 / 4 618 / 6 966 / 80 949 ns per solve (median) at m = 2 / 4 / 8 / 11 / 47, against the phase-3 review's loaded 1 329–1 360 / 2 118–2 162 / 4 372–4 478 / 6 668–6 803 / 75 156–78 177 — the same band; the solver's linear path is untouched by this task (the bench hands its rail and ground over as the ideal sources phase 0 did, so `m` counts the same matrix). A quiet-machine re-measure of both stays open, as it was for phase 3.

Moved with the split, each with its reason in-test: `pwl_elements::elements_that_chase_each_other…` names its cluster `B.P` (`VDD` and `GND` are terminals, no longer members); `build_fixed_point::an_idle_drive_on_a_pin_without_a_drive_slot…` drops its two `PowerOut` cases. The four goldens: three unchanged, one re-blessed as above.

The STM1061 (Rev 2.0 copy, October 2005 — ST's Rev 4 was unreachable while this was written, the constants are the ones both revisions carry, the page numbers are Rev 2.0's): `V_TH−` 1.600 V, `V_HYST` 0.05·`V_TH−` → `V_TH+` 1.68 V, valid from 0.7 V, `R_OL` 294 Ω from 1.7 mA at 0.5 V and `V_CC` = 1 V, `t_PD` 25 µs / `t_PR` 30 µs as scheduled instants. **Every rail input is a `PowerIn` sense, not `Analog`** — the plan said Analog; measured against the ROM boot: an analog sense on `Common_LDOin` or `Common_VDD` forces a solve of the divider cluster (sourced through the divider from the stuck ground) on every pass, one escalation each against the 0 the boot is held to, and a `PowerIn` sense delivers the identical published state (`Analog(v)` at path 0 from the terminal that feeds it). §12 item 5's single-source rule retired the difference.

Both are `BuildTopology` handed to every `ComponentNetIo` the build and the live path wire, fixed at build.
   - **The reference is declared on `Component::references()`, beside `branches()`, not on `PinDecl`** — `PinReference { pin, reference }`, validated at board build like a branch (`PartClass::Registered` gained `references`). `PinDecl` has no `reference` field yet (§11) and adding one touches every literal — 130-odd in this repository and 24 in MaD's `p2iss`/`MaDSim` — for a phase (5, `PinRole`) that rewrites them anyway; the declaration moves onto the pin then. The Rail declares input → ground and output → (isolated ground | ground); the detector `VCC` → `VSS`; the ISO67xx each side's `VCC` → that side's first `GND`, which is what makes `IC14`'s orphan `GND2` a domain measured against nothing. `DESIGN.md` rule 2 carries the note.
